**EN:** The character studio adds a **Chamber view** toggle next to Identity/Appearance shortcuts. It shows one form card at a time, retaining the Vuxian portrait, derived stats and save action; the hero and inactive fields are visually hidden but **both form cards remain mounted**, preserving the local draft. Keyboard focus follows the chosen chamber (`aria-pressed`) and the full overview remains available. Ten-point budget validation and server-side save authorization remain unchanged, with no POST/PATCH or refetch caused by chamber navigation. New responsive Playwright and screenshot coverage at 320/390/1280, IT/EN, dark and reduced-motion. **Verified on main `7c41b20`:** [CI #43010895328](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43010895328) **314/314 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit and Docker success**, [Visual #43010895313](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43010895313) **115/115**, [screenshot artifact #13850198593](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43010895313/artifacts/13850198593), [Pages #43010895460](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43010895460) and [Live #43010895461](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43010895461) **success**. Tested scenarios cover preserved draft, invalid budget with the hidden card, no extra reads/writes from chamber navigation, keyboard focus and viewport. #189 complete; parent #158 remains open for other screens and manual UX review.

## Nuovo confronto per-request tra MSP/IAD/MXP / Same-SHA public gate recovered after mixed POP responses (#147 — aperta)

**IT:** Dopo i tre round di [sampling #43006970649](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970649) con **24/27 HTTP 502 dal POP `IAD`**, il successivo [Pi #43007879412](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43007879412) per `d53668b` ha completato ARM64, backup e deploy Pi con API healthy. Il **primo gate pubblico esterno è fallito** su `health` 502 nonostante i diagnostici del medesimo giro da POP `MSP` mostrassero **`health 200`, `version 502`, `providers 200`, `social OPTIONS 502`, due history GET 401 e OPTIONS 204, bridge 200**: comportamento per-richiesta non uniforme anche nello stesso POP. Dal namespace connector Raspberry, origin **HTTP 200** e URL pubblico **HTTP 200 dal POP `MXP`**, con API/connector sani e nessun OOM/restart inatteso. **Un solo rerun del job pubblico fallito sul medesimo SHA, senza nuovo deploy, è riuscito**, quindi run attempt 2 green, mentre il primo fallimento resta reale. Nessun cambiamento a tunnel, segreti, OAuth o policy dei gate. La causa resta non dimostrata; [#147](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/issues/147) è **Blocked**, con [guida per il gestore Cloudflare](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/blob/main/docs/engineering/cloudflare-public-edge-triage.md).

**EN:** Following three rounds of [sampling #43006970649](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970649) with **24/27 HTTP 502 responses from `IAD`**, [Pi #43007879412](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43007879412) at `d53668b` completed the ARM64 build, backup and healthy API deployment. The **first external release gate failed** with health 502, yet independent diagnostics in the same round from `MSP` showed **health 200, version 502, providers 200, social OPTIONS 502, both private history GET 401 and OPTIONS 204, bridge 200**: the same POP had mixed per-request results. Within Raspberry's connector namespace, origin was **HTTP 200** and public API **HTTP 200 from `MXP`**, with healthy containers and no unexpected OOM/restarts. **Exactly one rerun of the failed public job, on the same SHA without redeployment, succeeded**, making attempt 2 green while the initial failure remains valid evidence. No changes were made to the tunnel, secrets, OAuth or strict release gates. Root cause is unproven; [#147](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/issues/147) remains **Blocked** with the [Cloudflare owner guide](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/blob/main/docs/engineering/cloudflare-public-edge-triage.md).

## Campionamento pubblico Cloudflare / Read-only public edge sampling (#188 — verificato)

**IT:** Per distinguere l'intermittenza API #147 dalle release del Raspberry, è stata aggiunta una diagnostica GitHub-hosted **senza segreti**, eseguibile manualmente, con **tre giri di nove probe** (health, versione, discovery provider, preflight OAuth, GET anonimi e preflight della storia privata, redirect Microsoft). Ogni risposta registra esclusivamente **HTTP status**, categoria server e suffisso POP Cloudflare validato; errori di rete sono ridotti a categorie fisse. Il file JSON di evidenze è sanificato e conservato per 7 giorni. Un singolo 502/503/504 o un 200 invece del 401 privato fa fallire il workflow; non vengono mai inviati JWT, cookie o identificativi di gioco. Il gate release rigoroso per commit/CORS/provider **rimane invariato**. Nessun calendario automatico per non aggravare la quota GitHub Actions #168. Lo stesso runner non simula più regioni: anche campioni tutti verdi non dimostrano stabilità multi-POP né chiudono #147. **Verificato con evidenze vere:** [workflow #43006970649](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970649) ha eseguito **3×9=27** prove, **24 HTTP 502 API dal POP `IAD`** e **3/3 bridge frontend HTTP 200**, con [artifact sanificato #13848672449](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970649/artifacts/13848672449): stato **failure corretto**, senza nascondere il guasto. Test offline tutti verdi. [CI #43006970584](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970584), [Pages #43006970525](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970525) e [Live #43006970557](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970557) **success**. Una prima configurazione YAML falliva senza job per il `:` non quotato nel nome dello step; corretta in `d53668b`. Runbook: `docs/engineering/cloudflare-public-edge-triage.md`. #188 completata; #147 **Blocked** per root cause/telemetria Cloudflare e stabilità multi-POP.

**EN:** To separate intermittent Cloudflare API #147 from Raspberry deployments, a no-secret GitHub-hosted diagnostic now runs on demand, taking **three passes over nine fixed public probes** (health, version, provider discovery, OAuth preflight, anonymous private-history GET and preflights, Microsoft redirect). Only HTTP status, allowlisted server class and validated Cloudflare POP suffix are logged; network errors use fixed categories. A sanitized JSON artifact is retained for 7 days. Any 502/503/504, or 200 instead of private 401, makes the workflow fail; no JWT, cookie or gameplay ID is ever sent. The strict deployment commit/CORS/provider gate is **unchanged**. No automatic schedule is added while Actions quota #168 is unresolved. A single hosted runner cannot simulate multiple regions: even fully green rounds neither prove multi-POP stability nor close #147. **Verified on real external traffic:** [workflow #43006970649](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970649) completed **3×9=27** probes: **24 API HTTP 502 responses from `IAD`**, while **3/3 frontend Microsoft bridges returned 200**, with [sanitized artifact #13848672449](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970649/artifacts/13848672449). Workflow **failure is the correct alert**, not swallowed; offline Node tests all passed. [CI #43006970584](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970584), [Pages #43006970525](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970525) and [Live #43006970557](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43006970557) **success**. An initial YAML definition had no jobs because a step name contained an unquoted colon; `d53668b` fixed it. Operator guide: `docs/engineering/cloudflare-public-edge-triage.md`. #188 complete; #147 remains **Blocked** pending Cloudflare root cause, telemetry and multi-POP stability.

## Cloudflare 502 dal DFW e recupero del gate hosted / DFW edge 502 then hosted gate recovery (#147 — ancora aperta)

**IT:** Sul rilascio [Pi #43003860875](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43003860875) (codice `9ca154d`) il build ARM64, backup e deploy Raspberry sono **success**, ma il primo gate pubblico GitHub-hosted ha visto **HTTP 502** su health/version/providers, social CORS preflight ed entrambe le history/private route+preflight dal POP **`DFW`**. La diagnostica read-only dello stesso ciclo mostra container API e connettore sani, origine **HTTP 200** dal namespace del connettore, **HTTP 200 su URL pubblico dal POP `MXP`**, senza OOM o restart inattesi. Un singolo retry del job esterno fallito, con stesso SHA, ha superato `health, commit, providers, CORS, private history auth, MSAL bridge` (run al tentativo 2 **success**). **Non equivale a stabilità multi-POP**: la #147 resta **Blocked/open** per diagnosi Cloudflare (hostname, routing, tunnel e telemetria) e smoke ripetibili, senza indebolire gate HTTP 200/401/CORS o cambiare segreti.

**EN:** In [Pi release #43003860875](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43003860875) (application SHA `9ca154d`), ARM64 build, backup and Raspberry deploy were **successful**, but the initial external GitHub-hosted gate observed **HTTP 502** for health/version/providers, social CORS preflight and both private history GET/preflights via Cloudflare colo **`DFW`**. Read-only Pi diagnostics from the same cycle confirm healthy API and tunnel containers, origin **HTTP 200** from the connector namespace, **HTTP 200 on the public endpoint via `MXP`**, no unexpected OOM or restarts. One retry of the failed external job against the same SHA passed `health, commit, providers, CORS, private history auth, MSAL bridge` (attempt 2 overall **success**). This is **not proof of multi-POP stability**: #147 remains **Blocked/open** pending Cloudflare hostname/routing/tunnel telemetry and repeated public verification, without weakening 200/401/CORS gates or changing secrets.

## Tastiera per sfide rune / Rune challenge keyboard controls (#187 — verificato)

**IT:** La sfida dei portali ora accetta i tasti **1–4** per scegliere Sole/Luna/Stella/Onda, **Backspace** per annullare una runa e **Invio** per confermare solo la sequenza completa, quando la **fase di risposta** è attiva e la **tastiera è focalizzata nell'arena**. Durante la preview, nei controlli nativi, con i modificatori o dopo una conferma/errore le scorciatoie non si attivano. Il pad touch rimane; l'arena è accessibile e spiega i tasti in italiano e inglese, con focus visibile. Un lock sincrono evita che doppio Invio/tap produca due POST prima della risposta; premi, danni e consumo delle assegnazioni restano esclusivamente verificati dal server. La regressione aggiuntiva blocca la risposta POST per simulare tre pressioni di Invio ravvicinate: **un solo invio** viene registrato. **Verificato su `9ca154d`:** [CI #43002895487](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895487) **311/311 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit e Docker success**; [Visual #43002895522](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895522) **115/115** con [screenshot #13846767816](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895522/artifacts/13846767816), [Pages #43002895666](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895666) e [Live #43002895501](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895501) **success**. #187 completata; #158 resta aperta per le altre schermate e QA umano.

**EN:** Portal challenges now accept **1–4** to choose Sun/Moon/Star/Wave, **Backspace** to undo and **Enter** to submit the complete sequence, strictly while the **answer phase** is active and the keyboard is **focused within the arena**. Hotkeys do not run during preview, from native controls, with modifier keys, or after an error/completion. Touch buttons remain usable; the keyboard arena has a visible focus state and localized IT/EN hints. A synchronous lock prevents rapid double Enter/tap from sending two POSTs before the response; the server alone verifies rewards, damage and consumed grants. An additional regression holds a POST response while three rapid Enter presses occur: **exactly one request** is sent. **Verified at `9ca154d`:** [CI #43002895487](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895487) **311/311 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit and Docker success**; [Visual #43002895522](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895522) **115/115** with [screenshots #13846767816](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895522/artifacts/13846767816); [Pages #43002895666](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895666) and [Live #43002895501](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/43002895501) **success**. #187 complete; parent #158 remains open for other screens and human QA.

## Pannelli focalizzati del Rifugio / Refuge focused chamber view (#186 — verificato)

**IT:** Dal Rifugio è ora possibile passare dalla panoramica completa alla **Vista a pannelli**: il menu interno seleziona una sola sezione (Attività, Salute, Preparazione o Cure), nasconde il lungo sommario senza spostare il documento e mantiene la navigazione dentro al GameDeck. Il focus tastiera viene portato al pannello scelto e il comando attivo è segnalato con `aria-pressed`; il vecchio salto alle sezioni resta disponibile in vista completa. Le snapshot private salute/equipaggiamento restano montate e provengono dalle API del proprietario: nessun nuovo endpoint, ricompensa o refetch causato dal cambio vista. Logout/cambio account cancella il focus. IT/EN, telefoni da 320px, dark e reduced-motion coperti da Playwright e screenshot 390/1280. **Verificato sul main `591c14c`:** [CI #42989086634](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086634) **308/308 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit + Docker success**, [Visual #42989086735](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086735) **115/115**, [artifact screenshot #13840863006](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086735/artifacts/13840863006), [Pages #42989086523](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086523) e [Live #42989086661](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086661) **success**. La prima CI `365ea3b` ha rilevato 307 pass, 1 fail per transizione MUI non annullata su riduci movimento; corretta da `591c14c` e verificata verde. #186 completata, #158 resta aperta per ulteriori pannelli/QA.

**EN:** Refuge now offers a **Chamber view** alongside the existing full overview: the sticky menu selects one section (Activities, Health, Preparation or Future Care), hiding the long summary without moving the document, with navigation still inside GameDeck. Keyboard focus moves to the selected panel and `aria-pressed` exposes the chosen command; full overview retains the original section jumps. Private health/equipment snapshots remain mounted and are fetched only from owner APIs, with no new endpoints, rewards or view-change refetch. Logout/account switching resets focus. IT/EN, 320px phones, dark mode and reduced motion are covered by Playwright and 390/1280 screenshot evidence. **Verified on main `591c14c`:** [CI #42989086634](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086634) **308/308 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit + Docker success**; [Visual #42989086735](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086735) **115/115**, [screenshot artifact #13840863006](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086735/artifacts/13840863006); [Pages #42989086523](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086523) and [Live #42989086661](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42989086661) **success**. The initial `365ea3b` CI found 307 passes and one real reduced-motion button transition failure; `591c14c` fixed it, and the final gate is green. #186 complete, parent #158 remains open for further UX work.

## Recupero discovery social su errori edge / Social discovery retry on edge failures (#185 — verificato)

**IT:** La pagina di accesso può ora recuperare una risposta **502/503/504** temporanea durante il solo `GET /api/auth/providers`: massimo **tre richieste totali** con due pause brevi (350/900 ms), annullabili quando l'utente cambia pagina o si autentica altrove. I provider rimangono in caricamento fino a un risultato attendibile; se il guasto persiste riappaiono messaggio localizzato e pulsante *Riprova* IT/EN esistenti. Nessun retry per HTTP 401/403, JSON non valido, CORS/network error o per il POST che scambia credenziali OAuth; zero token salvati/esposti. Playwright copre recupero, limite, errore permanente, cancel e singolo exchange. Questo **non risolve** il 502 intermittente multi-POP #147 né prova il login Google/Microsoft con account reali. La pipeline visuale esegue anche `auth.spec.ts`, allegando screenshot del recupero e dello stato d'errore a 390px. **Verificato su main `d163821`:** [CI #42985932237](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985932237) **305/305 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit e Docker success**; [Visual #42985930964](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985930964) **115/115** con [artifact screenshot #13839527673](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985930964/artifacts/13839527673); [Pages #42985931004](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985931004) e [Live #42985931160](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985931160) **success**. Il test finale riproduce tre risposte **502** consecutive e poi il recupero manuale. #185 completata; #147/#136/#139 restano aperte.

**EN:** Sign-in can now recover a transient **502/503/504** on the `GET /api/auth/providers` discovery only: at most **three total requests** with short 350/900 ms backoffs, aborted when navigation or cross-tab sign-in cancels the obsolete effect. The existing loading state remains until a trusted response arrives; persistent failures still expose the localized error and IT/EN manual *Try again*. HTTP 401/403, malformed JSON, CORS/network failures and OAuth credential exchange POST are never retried; no token is exposed or persisted. Playwright covers recovery, bounded failure, permanent error, cancellation and single exchange. This does **not** resolve regional Cloudflare #147 or verify real Google/Microsoft consumer login. Visual review also runs `auth.spec.ts` and saves screenshots of recovery and the 390px error state. **Verified on main `d163821`:** [CI #42985932237](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985932237) **305/305 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit and Docker success**; [Visual #42985930964](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985930964) **115/115** with [screenshot artifact #13839527673](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985930964/artifacts/13839527673); [Pages #42985931004](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985931004) and [Live #42985931160](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985931160) **success**. Final regression reproduces three consecutive **502** responses followed by successful manual retry. #185 complete; #147/#136/#139 remain open.

## Nuova ricorrenza 502 nello stesso POP / Mixed 502 and 401 within one Cloudflare colo (#147 — aperta)

**IT:** [Pi #42985076742](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985076742) su `9de4f03`: build/deploy ARM64, backup, API healthy e origine dal namespace Cloudflare **HTTP 200**. Il controllo GitHub-hosted fallisce su health/version/providers e diverse route con **HTTP 502 dal POP `DFW`**, ma **la route privata `/history/outcomes` restituisce HTTP 401 dallo stesso POP** nello stesso ciclo: il problema varia per richiesta e non dimostra un POP totalmente inattivo. La diagnostica Pi read-only vede API pubblica **HTTP 200 dal POP `MXP`**, senza OOM o restart inattesi. Un solo retry del job pubblico fallito è stato richiesto; i relativi esiti vanno verificati separatamente. La causa tecnica è ancora **sconosciuta**; #147 rimane **Blocked** per telemetria/routing/hostname Cloudflare e smoke ripetibili. Il retry frontend #185 attenua solo gli errori temporanei di discovery.

**EN:** [Pi #42985076742](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42985076742) at `9de4f03`: ARM64 build/deploy, backup, API health and connector-network origin **HTTP 200**. GitHub-hosted verification failed with **HTTP 502 from Cloudflare colo `DFW`** for health/version/providers and several routes, yet private `/history/outcomes` returned **HTTP 401 from the same colo** in the same diagnostic round. This varies per request and does not prove a completely unavailable POP. Read-only Pi diagnostics observed public **HTTP 200 via `MXP`**, without OOM or unexpected connector restarts. Exactly one retry of the failed public job was requested; its result is tracked independently. Root cause is still **unknown**; #147 remains **Blocked** pending owner Cloudflare telemetry/hostname/ingress checks and repeatable external smokes. #185 only mitigates transient browser discovery errors.

## Hover luminoso dei portali / Portal pointer glow (#184 — verificato)

**IT:** Il portale runico personale già concesso dal server ora reagisce al passaggio del mouse: alone e luce centrale si intensificano e si normalizzano su `pointerout`. Il cerchio cliccabile Phaser rimane fisso a raggio **19px**, senza ingrandire il target, cambiare posizione, concedere altri portali o alterare l'autorità API. Il cambiamento live di *riduci movimento* congela l'effetto anche mentre il puntatore resta sul portale. [Pi #42749088508](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42749088508) ARM64/deploy success, controllo pubblico intermittente #147 (fallimento iniziale e retry verde sul medesimo SHA). **EN:** Server-granted runic portals now react to mouse hover with brighter decorative halo and center, reverting on `pointerout`. The existing Phaser hit circle remains fixed at **19px** with no larger interaction area, position changes, extra portal grants or client-owned gameplay state. Live *reduced motion* freezes the glow even while hovered. New Playwright regression uses real mouse movement, leave both the portal and the canvas, click and 1280px desktop/390px mobile screenshot evidence; visual review already includes the portal spec. Initial [visual #42746708027](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42746708027) exposed a real defect (98 pass, 1 fail): a game-object `pointerout` does not fire when the pointer leaves the canvas; the scene now resets decorative hover via `GAME_OUT`. Second [visual #42747513336](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42747513336) passed 98 tests but the new mobile scenario sampled the marker during viewport resize (x=560 on a 388px canvas). The test now reloads at mobile size and waits for a visible projected marker before hovering. **Verified on main `a5fcf9f`:** [CI #42748199283](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42748199283) **301/301 Playwright, 487/487 xUnit and Docker success**; [Visual #42748199191](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42748199191) **99/99** with [screenshot artifact #13732197311](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42748199191/artifacts/13732197311); [Pages #42748199320](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42748199320) and [Live #42748199213](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42748199213) **success**. [Pi #42749088508](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42749088508) ARM64/deploy passed; its public gate was intermittent (#147: initial failure, green retry on identical SHA). #184 complete; manual art/device checks #167/#154/#165/#155 remain open.

## Ricorrenza POP Cloudflare / Regional Cloudflare recurrence (#147 — aperta)

**IT:** Il success del controllo pubblico [Pi #42733548716](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42733548716) era reale ma non stabile. Il nuovo deploy [Pi #42749088508](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42749088508) sul commit `a5fcf9f` supera build ARM64, backup, API healthy, connessione connector→origin HTTP 200 e riavvio `cloudflared`; **il primo controllo GitHub-hosted restituisce HTTP 502 su otto route API** dai POP Cloudflare `SJC` e `SEA`, mentre la diagnosi read-only sul Pi rileva **HTTP 200** per origin e API pubblica dal POP `MXP`, senza OOM o restart inattesi. Il **retry del solo job fallito**, stesso commit e senza nuovo deploy, è **success** (`PASS public health, commit, providers, CORS, private history auth, MSAL bridge`). La differenza tra POP è osservata, ma non prova una causa specifica di edge o tunnel. #147 è **riaperta/Blocked** per verifica routing/hostname, propagazione e telemetria Cloudflare da parte del gestore; non modificare segreti o bypassare gate HTTP 200/CORS/401. Login con account reali Google/Microsoft resta fuori da questa prova (#136/#139).

**EN:** The successful public check in [Pi #42733548716](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42733548716) was genuine but not consistently repeatable. New [Pi #42749088508](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42749088508) for `a5fcf9f` passed ARM64 build, backup, API health, connector→origin HTTP 200 and `cloudflared` restart; its **first independent GitHub-hosted probe returned HTTP 502 across eight API routes** from Cloudflare colos `SJC`/`SEA`. Read-only Raspberry diagnostics saw **origin and public HTTP 200** from colo `MXP`, with no OOM or unexpected connector restart. **Retrying only the failed gate** on the *same commit without redeployment* returned **success** (`PASS public health, commit, providers, CORS, private history auth, MSAL bridge`). POP differences are observations, not proof of an exact tunnel/edge root cause. #147 is **reopened/Blocked** pending owner Cloudflare hostname/routing/propagation and telemetry checks; do not change secrets or relax HTTP 200/CORS/401 gates. Real consumer Google/Microsoft login is not established by this smoke (#136/#139).

## Recupero API pubblico / Public API recovery (#147 — precedente recupero verificato)

**IT:** Il problema HTTP 502 segnalato dagli agent GitHub esterni è rientrato nel [deploy Pi #42733548716](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42733548716), che pubblica l'immagine ARM64 del commit `d1af35a`. Il Pi ha superato backup PostgreSQL verificato, health API e comunicazione connector-origine; dopo il riavvio del processo `cloudflared` già previsto dallo script, il job esterno `verify-public-auth` ha stampato **PASS public health, commit, providers, CORS, private history auth, MSAL bridge**. Test di codice [CI #42732771161](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42732771161) verdi. Il workaround infrastrutturale è nel deploy e coperto dai test bash precedenti; nessun gate, credenziale, database o impostazione Cloudflare modificato. La causa esatta del 502 precedente non è provata (il refresh del tunnel è un indizio, non una certezza), e la durata della stabilità va monitorata. **Non** è un test di login Google/Microsoft con account consumer reali: issue OAuth dedicate restano aperte.

**EN:** The GitHub-hosted external HTTP 502 failure has cleared in [Pi deployment #42733548716](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42733548716), which published ARM64 revision `d1af35a`. Verified PostgreSQL backup, API health and connector-to-origin checks passed; after the deploy script's existing `cloudflared` restart, the independent hosted `verify-public-auth` printed **PASS public health, commit, providers, CORS, private history auth, MSAL bridge**. Code test [CI #42732771161](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42732771161) is green. No release gate, credential, database or Cloudflare configuration was weakened. Restarting the tunnel coincided with recovery but does not establish the previous 502 root cause or long-term stability; monitor for recurrence. Real Google/Microsoft consumer account sign-in remains unverified under separate open issues.

## Confronto POP Cloudflare / Cloudflare datacenter comparison (#147 — in verifica)

**IT:** Il deploy ARM64 [#42730913630](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730913630) ha superato build/backup/health origine, ma i controlli esterni vedono ancora **HTTP 502**, tutti dal POP `LAX`, mentre la prova HTTP pubblica dal namespace del connector Pi dà **200**. La diagnosi read-only del Pi ora può aggiungere il **solo codice POP di tre lettere** del `CF-Ray` alla prova pubblica già esistente, senza query aggiuntive né stampa dell'ID di richiesta, IP, header completi, body o segreti. Test Python riproducono intestazioni valide/corrotte, controlli di allowlist, timeout e pulizia log. Nessuna modifica a Cloudflare, a OAuth, a database o al gate di release: causa ancora non dimostrata. La [CI #42732771161](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42732771161) ha convalidato i nuovi test Python, ma [Pi #42733055348](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42733055348) ha bloccato il deploy di una revisione non più corrente prima del probe, quindi il confronto POP reale resta da raccogliere.

**EN:** ARM64 [run #42730913630](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730913630) passed build/backup/internal health, but public hosted checks still returned **HTTP 502** from Cloudflare colo `LAX`, while the Pi connector namespace observed public HTTP **200**. The Pi's existing read-only public probe now optionally returns *only* the three-letter validated `CF-Ray` datacenter suffix, without additional requests or exposing the complete ray ID, IPs, headers, bodies or secrets. Python tests cover valid/malformed headers, allowlisted targets, timeouts and redaction. No Cloudflare, OAuth, database or strict release gate changes; the root cause is still unknown. [CI #42732771161](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42732771161) validates the new Python tests, but [Pi #42733055348](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42733055348) rejected a stale revision before deploying or running the probe; a live Pi colo comparison is still pending.

## Archi runici dei portali / Illustrated portal thresholds (#183 — verificato)

**IT:** I portali personali già concessi dal server ricevono un disegno originale a pietra incisa, alone runico e luce centrale morbida. L'animazione modifica **solo** livelli ornamentali riutilizzati: il cerchio Phaser realmente cliccabile rimane a coordinate e raggio costanti anziché pulsare. La preferenza *riduci movimento* congela la luce, anche se attivata durante il gioco. Nessun nuovo marker, premio, collisione, richiesta API, asset esterno o privilegio browser. ## Diagnostica Cloudflare con datacenter minimizzato / Minimal Cloudflare colo diagnostic (#147 — test offline superati, gate pubblico bloccato)

**IT:** Il check pubblico dalla macchina GitHub continua a riportare HTTP 502 per health/version/provider/CORS/storico, mentre dal namespace connettore Raspberry origin e URL pubblico danno HTTP 200 (Pi #42725879992). Per confrontare due punti di osservazione il diagnostico HTTP non bloccante stampa, *solo se validato*, il suffisso di tre lettere del datacenter nell'header `CF-Ray`, **mai** l'identificativo completo, headers, IP, body o token. I test offline includono CF-Ray validi, corrotti e non Cloudflare; il controllo pubblicazione HTTP 200/CORS/401 non cambia e rimane rosso fino a prove reali. Nessuna modifica a Cloudflare, credenziali, API o database. La causa del 502 non è dimostrata.

**EN:** GitHub-hosted public probes still see Cloudflare HTTP 502 across API routes while the Pi connector namespace observes HTTP 200 for both origin and public URL (Pi #42725879992). The read-only HTTP diagnostic now emits *only* the allowlisted three-letter Cloudflare datacenter suffix from a validated `CF-Ray`, never the complete request ID, headers, IP, response body or tokens. Offline tests cover valid, malformed and non-Cloudflare values. Strict public HTTP 200/CORS/401 release gates remain unchanged and red until independently verified; no Cloudflare settings, secrets, API or database changes. No definitive 502 root cause is claimed.

**IT:** La camminata del mondo #182 è stata pubblicata nella [run Pages #42730121897](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730121897): artifact, build e deploy **success** al primo tentativo per SHA `880309f`, URL `https://vuxo.quest` (`Reported success!`). [CI #42730122118](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730122118) **success** (299 Playwright, 487 xUnit, Docker e diagnostica pubblica offline), [Live #42730121869](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730121869) **success**; la matrice [Visual #42725101278](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725101278) era 79/79 sul medesimo codice funzionale. **#182 conclusa**; review artistica e dispositivo fisico rimangono nelle parent. **#180 resta aperta** per investigare i due timeout della precedente run `a0e8696`: il suo artefatto non è mai diventato success dopo i retry; la nuova pubblicazione non dimostra una root cause né correzione permanente.

**EN:** World stride #182 is included in the successful first-attempt [Pages #42730121897](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730121897) deployment of SHA `880309f` to `https://vuxo.quest` (`Reported success!`). [CI #42730122118](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730122118) **succeeded** (299 Playwright, 487 xUnit, Docker and the new redacted public-diagnostic offline test), [Live #42730121869](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42730121869) **succeeded**, and [Visual #42725101278](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725101278) passed 79/79 for the same functional source. **#182 completed**; physical/art review remains tracked by parent issues. **#180 stays open** to investigate repeated timeouts of the older `a0e8696` deployment: its artifact never succeeded on retries, and the new successful deployment does not establish root cause or permanent platform recovery.

## Camminata illustrata / Illustrated character stride (#182 — verificata)

**IT:** Gli avatar originali del mondo hanno un piccolo movimento di camminata: mantello e passo si muovono soltanto mentre la silhouette segue una **posizione autorevole ricevuta dal server**, con traccia decorativa riutilizzata senza particelle aggiuntive. La radice dell'avatar, la telecamera, le etichette, la collisione e il ribaltamento sinistra/destra restano invariati. La preferenza *riduci movimento* interrompe subito ogni animazione e può cambiare anche durante l'esplorazione. Nessuna nuova API, premio, velocità o stato gameplay lato browser. Nuova regressione Playwright mobile verifica camminata, riposo, cambio della preferenza e avanzamento server anche a movimento ridotto; screenshot E2E allegato. Primo tentativo [CI #42724320964](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42724320964) e [Visual #42724321006](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42724321006): tutti i vecchi test passavano, ma il nuovo test campionava un frame transitorio e falliva; non è stato eliminato. Fix `a0e8696`: 12 frame di passo dopo aggiornamento *server-owned* e contatori diagnostici E2E. [CI #42725100997](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725100997) **299/299 Playwright + 487/487 xUnit e Docker success**; [Visual #42725101278](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725101278) **79/79**, [Live #42725101062](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725101062) success. [Pages #42725100953](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725100953): build success, primo deploy **syncing_files timeout**, retry del solo job **fallito nuovamente** dopo timeout in `finished_file_sync` (#180). **#182 rimane aperta fino alla pubblicazione confermata.**

**EN:** Original world avatars gain a lightweight illustrated walking stride: cloak/body and a reused decorative footfall move only while interpolating toward a **server-authoritative position**. The avatar root, follow camera, labels, collisions and horizontal facing are unchanged. The *reduced motion* preference immediately disables the animation, including changes during exploration. No new API, reward, speed or browser-authored gameplay state. A new mobile Playwright regression checks walking, idle, live preference switching and continued server movement with reduced motion; E2E screenshot attached. Initial [CI #42724320964](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42724320964) and [Visual #42724321006](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42724321006): prior tests passed, but the new test sampled a transient frame and failed; it was not removed. Fix `a0e8696`: 12 rendered stride frames after an authoritative target change and cumulative E2E-only observation. [CI #42725100997](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725100997) **299/299 Playwright + 487/487 xUnit and Docker success**, [Visual #42725101278](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725101278) **79/79**, [Live #42725101062](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725101062) success. [Pages #42725100953](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725100953): build success, first deployment **syncing_files timeout**, deploy-job-only retry **failed again** after a `finished_file_sync` timeout (#180). **#182 remains open until publishing succeeds.**

## Radar tattico esplorazione / Tactical exploration radar (#181 — verificato)

**IT:** La schermata del mondo ha una minimappa tattica originale, centrata sul proprio Vuxian. Mostra gli altri giocatori nel raggio di 12 caselle e le soglie personali provenienti **soltanto** dai grant autorizzati; una soglia più lontana compare sul bordo, senza inventare la sua posizione o consentire ingressi fittizi. La vista si aggiorna dagli stessi snapshot realtime e dai portali esistenti, senza chiamate extra, teletrasporto, premi o autorità client. Su mobile/schermi bassi è richiudibile, con controlli accessibili, IT/EN, tema e reduced-motion. Nuove regressioni Playwright E2E e screenshot owner fake mobile/desktop. [CI #42722064119](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722064119) **298/298 Playwright e 487/487 xUnit**, backend e Docker runtime success; [Visual #42722064297](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722064297) **78/78**, inclusi i due screenshot con radar a 390px/1280px; [Pages #42722063850](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722063850) e [Live #42722063788](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722063788) success. **#181 completata**; review su hardware reale e fullscreen #154/#155/#165 restano aperte. La API pubblica Cloudflare #147 non è considerata guarita.

**EN:** The world gains an original tactical minimap centered on the player. It displays other explorers within a 12-tile radius and personal portals from **authorized owner-only grants**; a distant gateway appears on the perimeter rather than fabricating its position or granting entry. It updates from existing realtime/world portal data without extra calls, teleportation, rewards or client-side authority. Collapsible on narrow/short screens, accessible, IT/EN, themed and reduced-motion friendly. Added Playwright multiplayer/privacy/mobile regressions and owner-safe visual screenshots. [CI #42722064119](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722064119) **298/298 Playwright plus 487/487 xUnit**, backend and Docker runtime success; [Visual #42722064297](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722064297) **78/78**, including two radar screenshots at 390px/1280px; [Pages #42722063850](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722063850) and [Live #42722063788](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42722063788) success. **#181 completed**; physical device/fullscreen and art review #154/#155/#165 remains open. The independent Cloudflare public API #147 is not considered healthy.

## Recupero e ricorrenza Pages / Pages recovery and recurrence (#180 — riaperta)

**IT — ricorrenza 10/11:** Nuovo timeout `syncing_files` sull'artefatto `a0e8696` della [run #42725100953](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725100953), nonostante build validata. Issue #180 riaperta e ritentato **solo** il job deploy; nessuna prova che la causa sia nel frontend o in un incidente generalizzato GitHub Pages. Anche il secondo tentativo è terminato **failure** dopo oltre dieci minuti in `finished_file_sync`; #180 ora `Blocked`. Verificare Pages/environment/deployment via console e non avviare retry illimitati: release web `a0e8696` non confermata.

**EN — recurrence Nov 10:** The same `syncing_files` timeout recurred for artifact `a0e8696` in [Pages #42725100953](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42725100953), despite a successful build. Issue #180 reopened and a **deployment-job-only** retry started. No evidence identifies an application defect or a platform-wide incident. The second attempt also **failed** after over ten minutes in `finished_file_sync`; #180 is now `Blocked`. Inspect GitHub Pages/environment deployment state before any bounded retry; the `a0e8696` web release is not confirmed.

**IT:** Durante il rilascio touch #179, l'artifact Pages e la build del commit `c457075` erano corretti, ma il job deploy della [run #42718383066](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383066) è rimasto in `syncing_files` per ~10 minuti e ha fallito per timeout, senza pubblicare quel tentativo. Un **retry del solo job deploy** ha completato `Reported success!` su `https://vuxo.quest` (attempt 2) con il **medesimo artifact e SHA**, senza nuove modifiche, token o configurazioni. Non è dimostrata una causa applicativa o un incidente globale GitHub Pages; #180 chiusa dopo retry riuscito. Il gate API pubblico su Raspberry rimane separatamente rosso HTTP 502 (#147).

**EN:** The #179 Pages build/artifact for `c457075` was valid, but [run #42718383066](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383066) stalled at `syncing_files` for about ten minutes, timed out and did not publish on its first attempt. A **deployment-job-only retry** reported success to `https://vuxo.quest` on attempt 2 using the **same artifact and SHA**, without changing source, token or settings. Neither an application root cause nor a platform-wide Pages incident is proven; #180 closed after verified recovery. The independent Pi public API gate still fails HTTP 502 (#147).

## Touch sicuro durante duello, blur e reconnect / Touch input lock during duels, blur and reconnect (#179 — verificato)

**IT:** I quattro controlli touch del mondo ora rispettano lo stesso blocco della tastiera: diventano disabilitati quando la connessione non è online, quando è aperto un portale/duello o quando la finestra non è attiva. Un blur interrompe immediatamente l'intent attivo, e il ritorno al focus non ricomincia il movimento senza una nuova pressione. La guardia a runtime impedisce eventi sintetici/stale che bypassano la UI disabilitata. Nessun cambiamento a velocità, posizioni o ricompense server-owned. Nuovi test mobile Playwright per dialog, focus e reconnect. [CI #42718383036](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383036) **296/296 Playwright e 487/487 xUnit**, Docker runtime success; [Visual #42718383096](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383096) **78/78**, [Live #42718383304](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383304) success; [Pages #42718383066](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383066) **secondo tentativo riuscito** dopo il timeout di pubblicazione #180. #179 chiusa, #155 fullscreen hardware ancora da verificare.

**EN:** All four world touch directions now honor the keyboard's input lock: they are disabled while disconnected, during a portal duel/dialog, or while the window is unfocused. Browser blur immediately clears an active movement intent; restoring focus never restarts travel without a fresh press. A runtime guard also rejects stale/synthetic pointer events that bypass disabled UI. Server-owned speed, positions and rewards are unchanged. New mobile Playwright tests cover dialogs, focus and reconnect. [CI #42718383036](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383036) **296/296 Playwright and 487/487 xUnit**, Docker runtime success; [Visual #42718383096](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383096) **78/78**, [Live #42718383304](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383304) success; [Pages #42718383066](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42718383066) **successful on second deployment attempt** after #180's publishing timeout. #179 closed; #155 physical fullscreen QA remains open.

## Bussola portali personali / Personal portal compass (#178 — verificato)

**IT:** Una piccola bussola nel HUD del mondo indica il portale *personale autorizzato* più vicino, con orientamento in otto direzioni e distanza approssimata in caselle. Si aggiorna dai soli snapshot già ricevuti (posizione giocatore SignalR e grant portali owner-only), senza nuove richieste né privilegi o click automatici. Nessun obiettivo, spawn, ricompensa, collisione o coordinate sono inventati dal browser. Sul cellulare è compatta, con etichetta accessibile, lingua IT/EN e preferenze reduced motion. Regressioni Playwright: orientamento est/sud, movimento, cambio lingua, nessun portale guest, coordinate malformate. [CI #42716326716](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326716) **293 Playwright + 487 xUnit**, Docker success; [Visual #42716326583](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326583) **78 screenshot Playwright** inclusi i due nuovi quadri del mondo a 390/1280px; [Pages #42716326532](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326532) e [Live #42716326418](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326418) success. Primo tentativo CI #42715665777 aveva TS2769 MUI, corretto in `a5a8419`. **#178 completata**; review fisica e art direction #154/#155/#165 restano separate.

**EN:** A compact world HUD compass points to the nearest *authorized personal portal*, with eight-way bearings and approximate tile distance. It updates solely from existing SignalR player snapshots and owner-only portal grants, adding no requests, privileges or automatic entry. The browser never invents quests, spawns, rewards, collisions or authoritative coordinates. Compact on mobile, accessible, localized IT/EN and reduced-motion friendly. New Playwright regressions cover east/south bearings, movement, language switch, no guest portals and malformed coordinates. [CI #42716326716](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326716) **293 Playwright + 487 xUnit**, Docker success; [Visual #42716326583](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326583) **78 visual Playwright cases** including the new 390/1280px owner-only compass screenshots; [Pages #42716326532](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326532) and [Live #42716326418](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42716326418) success. Initial CI #42715665777 identified MUI TS2769 and was corrected by `a5a8419`. **#178 complete**; physical-device/fullscreen/art direction review #154/#155/#165 remains separate.

## Input di movimento sicuro su blur e touch / Safe movement input on blur and touch (#177 — verificato)

**IT:** Il mondo interrompe l'intent di movimento quando si perde il focus della finestra o la scheda viene nascosta, azzerando i tasti Phaser rimasti premuti; al ritorno serve un nuovo input. I pulsanti direzionali touch fermano il movimento anche quando il puntatore lascia il controllo senza rilasciare sul pulsante. Le coordinate, le collisioni e le ricompense restano autorevoli sul server. Nuovi test Playwright: tastiera blur/refocus e uscita touch a 390px. [CI #42712674488](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674488) **290/290 Playwright e 487 xUnit**, backend/Docker verdi; [Visual #42712674267](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674267) **76/76**, [Pages #42712674686](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674686) **success**, [Live #42712674303](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674303) **3/3**. Issue #177 chiusa dopo verifica; #155/#154 QA fullscreen/hardware restano aperte.

**EN:** The world now sends neutral movement when browser focus is lost or the tab becomes hidden, clearing any stuck Phaser keys; movement needs a fresh input after focus returns. Touch direction controls also release movement when the pointer exits without a matching button-up event. Server-owned positions, collisions and rewards are unchanged. New Playwright regressions cover keyboard blur/refocus and pointer exit at 390px. [CI #42712674488](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674488) **290/290 Playwright plus 487 xUnit**, backend/Docker green; [Visual #42712674267](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674267) **76/76**, [Pages #42712674686](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674686) **success**, [Live #42712674303](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42712674303) **3/3**.
