## GHCR ARM64 push autorizzazione / GHCR ARM64 package authorization (#176 — recuperato)

**IT:** La [run Pi #42710479084](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710479084) del codice UX d26a857 compila il backend .NET per linux/arm64, ma il push del container GHCR fallisce con `permission_denied: read_package` nonostante `build-arm64` dichiari `packages: write`. I job deploy Pi, verify-public-auth e triage sono **skipped**: nessun aggiornamento del Pi e nessun nuovo gate pubblico in questa run. Indagine delle impostazioni package Actions access nel #176, senza divulgare token o aprire la visibilità del pacchetto. Il blocco di pubblicazione è **superato** nella [run Pi #42713542566](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42713542566): build e push GHCR ARM64 completati, deploy e backup validato, origin locale 200. La causa della vecchia denied e i permessi di Packages non sono stati letti direttamente: non attribuiamo la correzione a una specifica impostazione. Il gate pubblico da GitHub continua a fallire HTTP 502 (#147); non è un successo di rilascio.

**EN:** [Pi run #42710479084](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710479084) successfully compiled .NET for ARM64, but GHCR denied image upload with `permission_denied: read_package` despite the build job requesting `packages: write`. Pi deploy and public gate jobs were skipped; **no new production image was deployed**. Issue #176 tracks GHCR package/repository Actions access without exposing credentials or changing package visibility. The GHCR push blocker was **not reproduced** in [Pi run #42713542566](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42713542566): ARM64 build and push succeeded, Pi deployment and protected backup verified, internal origin HTTP 200. Original denial cause and package access settings were not independently inspected; do not infer which setting changed. The independent GitHub-hosted public release gate still fails HTTP 502 (#147).


## Viste Equipaggiamento, Zaino e Cronologia / Inventory game-first quick views (#175 — verified)

**IT:** La nuova slice #175 dell'EPIC #158, commit [`ddcd56b`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/commit/ddcd56b29c704a52895a0e7e9aaf2660d02f08b7), organizza la UI Inventario in quattro viste **Panoramica** (default, con rendering originale completo), **Equipaggiamento** (slot/loadout/statistiche), **Zaino** (ricerca, filtri, pile ed equip/uso oggetti) e **Cronologia** (grant e consumo in forma di diario). Sono filtri di presentazione che non chiamano API al cambio vista e non alterano owner, grantId, quantità, equip, HP o ricompense: le letture owner-scoped restano sempre il riferimento. Il pulsante *Trova oggetti compatibili* di uno slot apre lo Zaino con filtro per slot disponibile e focus ricerca dopo il render. Selezioni/ispezioni sono resettate alla chiusura della modale, e il cambio account remonta la pagina; le viste usano `aria-pressed`, tastiera, tema e traduzioni IT/EN. Il menu è sticky nel contenitore scorrevole interno al Game Deck, così il documento non va in scroll orizzontale. [Visual #42708138944](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708138944) **76/76 pass**, [artifact #13715306013](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708138944/artifacts/13715306013) comprende nuove immagini Gear/Bag/Journal a 390×844 e 1280×720 per IT/chiaro e EN/scuro, revisionate; [Pages #42708139050](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708139050) e [Live #42708138929](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708138929) success. [CI #42710599128](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599128) **288 Playwright pass**, backend/Docker success. Dopo un primo fallimento di tre test Playwright in #42708138973 (test legacy), `d26a857` ha aggiornato i percorsi test e `89bb94a` ha impedito ai filtri nascosti dello Zaino di nascondere grant consumati in Cronologia. [Visual #42710599429](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599429) **76 pass**; [Pages #42710599140](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599140) e [Live #42710599130](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599130) success. **#175 completata**, mentre #158/#165 restano aperte. #158 e #165 restano aperte per art direction e controlli device reali.

**EN:** #175 of EPIC #158 (commit [`ddcd56b`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/commit/ddcd56b29c704a52895a0e7e9aaf2660d02f08b7)) introduces four game-first owner inventory views: **Overview** (fully backward-compatible default), **Equipment** (loadout/statistics), **Backpack** (browse/filter, stacks and item actions), and **History** (earned grant journal). Switching views is presentation-only and triggers no gameplay API mutations or owner changes; all stock, equipment and grant IDs remain server-owned. Equipment slot *Find compatible items* enters the Backpack with a slot/availability filter and focuses the mounted search field. Item inspection and modes are safely scoped to the active authenticated owner, with keyboard activation, `aria-pressed`, IT/EN and theme support. The sticky view strip scrolls within the Game Deck without external document scroll. [Visual #42708138944](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708138944) **76 passed** and its [artifact #13715306013](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708138944/artifacts/13715306013) contains inspected Overview/Equipment/Backpack/History screenshots at 390×844 and 1280×720 in IT/light and EN/dark; [Pages #42708139050](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708139050) and [Live #42708138929](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42708138929) succeeded. [CI #42710599128](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599128) **288 Playwright passed**, backend/Docker successful; after the initial CI regression in #42708138973, `d26a857` corrected legacy keyboard/browse test paths and `89bb94a` made the full grant history independent of hidden Backpack filters. [Visual #42710599429](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599429) **76 passed**, [Pages #42710599140](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599140) and [Live #42710599130](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42710599130) succeeded. **#175 completed**; #158 and #165 remain open for broader art-direction/hardware QA. #158 and #165 remain open for broader art-direction/hardware QA.


## Visuale adattiva del mondo / Responsive world camera (#174 — QA in progress)

**IT:** Implementata direttamente su `main` con [c2b9dd3](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/c2b9dd3c681a6c0a74bcdcef993a5c8de0c04e4d) una camera Phaser da zoom **1× su smartphone a max 1,9× su desktop/ultrawide**, ricalcolata sulle dimensioni reali del canvas per orientamento, resize e fullscreen; limita l'ingrandimento conservando spazio per il mondo. Server, movement intent, tile autorevoli, interazioni portali, inventario e ricompense non sono modificati. La prima matrice [Visual #42700708361](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42700708361) aveva **76 pass**, ma analizzando gli screenshot a 1920/3440 è emerso che il terreno lasciava barre vuote a destra/in basso e la [CI #42700708466](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42700708466) ha dato **284 Playwright pass/2 fail** sui click portal marker. Fix [a449061](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/commit/a449061b5cf613f9dc346984dc46026eb49defb6): limiti disegno + proiezione portali usando la `camera.worldView` reale. La seconda Visual [#42702099520](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42702099520) è **76/76 pass** e i PNG wide mostrano ora terreno fino ai bordi; la [CI #42702099313](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42702099313) ha **285 pass/1 fail** nel test ultrawide perché la camera lerp continuava a muoversi mentre Playwright leggeva il marker con più chiamate lente. La trace del failure conferma x marker in movimento e click su una coordinata obsoleta. Fix [ca1b31f](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/commit/ca1b31f4367c1e4b1b472a0273295541ef347ca5) centra immediatamente il giocatore solo a join/resize e raccoglie marker+canvas in una sola valutazione, mantenendo lerp durante il movimento successivo. [Visual #42704351524](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351524) **76/76 pass**, [artifact screenshot](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351524/artifacts/13712303613); [Pages #42704351500](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351500) e [Live browser/API/PostgreSQL #42704351676](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351676) **success**. La CI [#42704351760](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351760) è **success: 286 test Playwright superati, backend e Docker verdi**. #174 resta aperta per QA manuale su dispositivi reali; #147 Cloudflare 502 indipendente.

**EN:** Commit [c2b9dd3](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/commit/c2b9dd3c681a6c0a74bcdcef993a5c8de0c04e4d) adds a viewport-native Phaser camera scaling **1× phone to at most 1.9× desktop/ultrawide** by actual canvas dimensions on resize/fullscreen, without changing authoritative player positions, collisions, portal hit areas, inventory or rewards. Initial [Visual #42700708361](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42700708361) passed **76** but inspecting 1920/3440 screenshots showed empty right/bottom terrain strips and [CI #42700708466](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42700708466) recorded **284 Playwright passed/2 failed** on portal marker clicks. Fix [a449061](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/commit/a449061b5cf613f9dc346984dc46026eb49defb6) uses actual `camera.worldView` for terrain and portal projection; [Visual #42702099520](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42702099520) **76 passed** and visual review confirmed full terrain coverage. [CI #42702099313](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42702099313) still had **285 passed/1 failed** on ultrawide because the follow camera kept easing while Playwright sampled marker position over several slow browser calls, so the click used a stale screen coordinate. Fix [ca1b31f](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/commit/ca1b31f4367c1e4b1b472a0273295541ef347ca5) instantly centers the follow target only at join/resize and samples marker+canvas bounds together; ordinary movement continues easing. [Visual #42704351524](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351524) **76/76 passed**, [screenshot artifact](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351524/artifacts/13712303613), [Pages #42704351500](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351500) and [Live #42704351676](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351676) **passed**. [CI #42704351760](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42704351760) **succeeded with 286 Playwright passed, backend and Docker green**. #174 remains open for human on-device review; the separate #147 Cloudflare 502 issue remains blocking for public backend.


## Character Studio e multiplayer: aggiornamento live concluso / Character Studio live multiplayer appearance complete (#169, #173)

**IT:** Il flusso end-to-end di aggiornamento del look dopo la creazione è stato completato su main. `GET /api/characters/me/appearance` legge la versione/appearance e l'editor blocca nome/statistiche già persistiti. `PATCH /api/characters/me/appearance` richiede `catalogVersion`, `expectedRevision` e soltanto i dieci campi cosmetici, con proprietario scelto dal JWT; `AppearanceRevision` PostgreSQL e CAS atomico impediscono overwrite concorrenti, mentre richieste identiche ripetute non generano revisioni o notifiche aggiuntive. Dopo commit il world server incrementa la propria revision e notifica `world.playerUpdated` agli altri partecipanti, scartando revisioni obsolete; reconnect ricarica il look persistito. Test finali: [CI #42695414456](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42695414456) **success, 282 Playwright, backend e Docker**; [Live E2E #42695413815](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42695413815) **success, 3 test browser/API/PostgreSQL/SignalR** incluso aggiornamento visto da due client connessi, retry, conflitto, owner e reconnect; [Visual #42693836368](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42693836368) **success, 75 test**; [Pages #42695414305](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42695414305) **success**. La prima CI ha fallito per `using Xunit` mancante nel nuovo test; la seconda per un vecchio fixture che simulava la creazione senza il GET iniziale. Entrambi corretti nei commit `87b830f` e `0ed801c` con copertura conservata. **#169 e #173 chiuse**; #167/#174 restano aperte per art direction e scala, #147/#59 per verifica Cloudflare/Raspberry. Nessuna prova di disponibilità pubblica aggiornata del backend viene dedotta dai test locali.

**EN:** The post-creation appearance edit workflow is complete on main. `GET /api/characters/me/appearance` loads the saved look/revision, and Character Studio locks persisted name/stats. `PATCH /api/characters/me/appearance` accepts only `catalogVersion`, `expectedRevision` and the ten allowed cosmetic fields, selecting the database owner from JWT claims. PostgreSQL `AppearanceRevision` and atomic compare-and-swap prevent concurrent overwrites; identical retries do not issue duplicate revisions/events. Once committed, the world server increments its own revision and sends `world.playerUpdated` to online peers, rejecting stale callbacks; reconnect reads persisted cosmetics. Final evidence: [CI #42695414456](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42695414456) **282 Playwright passed**, backend/Docker success; [Live E2E #42695413815](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42695413815) **3 real-browser/API/PostgreSQL/SignalR tests passed**, including two online peer views, idempotent replay, conflict, ownership isolation and reconnect; [Visual #42693836368](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42693836368) **75 passed**, [Pages #42695414305](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42695414305) succeeded. Initial CI failed on a missing `using Xunit` in the PostgreSQL test; a later run exposed an older create-character fixture that did not respond to the new initial GET. Both were corrected in `87b830f` and `0ed801c` without disabling checks. **#169 and #173 closed**; #167/#174 remain for visual/art scale, and #147/#59 for Cloudflare/Raspberry production verification. Local integration success is not proof of public API availability.


## Modifica aspetto persistita dopo la creazione / Post-creation character appearance updates (#173 — QA in corso)

**IT:** Il Character Studio ora carica il personaggio persistito autenticato tramite `GET /api/characters/me/appearance`, blocca nome/statistiche già assegnati e permette di cambiare solo i dieci attributi cosmetici previsti dal catalogo. `PATCH /api/characters/me/appearance` richiede `catalogVersion`, `expectedRevision` e l'appearance, senza alcun ownerId nel payload; l'identità JWT determina la riga da aggiornare. Una nuova migrazione `202611090001_CharacterAppearanceRevision` aggiunge un contatore bigint con default zero; un singolo `UPDATE ... WHERE OwnerId AND AppearanceRevision` aggiorna **soltanto i cosmetici** e incrementa la revisione, rilevando salvataggi concorrenti. Il replay dello stesso look è idempotente. Dopo il commit riuscito, il server aggiorna il `WorldSimulation` soltanto se characterId/revisione sono coerenti, incrementa la revision world e pubblica `world.playerUpdated` ai peer; join e reconnect leggono dal database. Un fallimento SignalR non finge il rollback del DB e il prossimo join riallinea il mondo. Il client aggiorna l'avatar Phaser dai campi versionati, senza inviare modifiche con movement intent. Commit [`d06b44a`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/d06b44a0c2f6c523e266b1f38b6d6e4668397dc6), [`87b830f`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/87b830f8dc76ad8551176a7928e03376eab7dbef), [`9db7ad0`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/9db7ad0ff2cd8ccd8dd8d1644a1853583fe99852). Un primo job backend CI è fallito per un semplice `using Xunit` mancante nel test PostgreSQL, corretto sul commit seguente. La run CI [#42694635157](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42694635157) e l'E2E [#42694635067](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42694635067) sono in verifica; la [Visual #42693836368](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42693836368) e [Pages #42694634612](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42694634612) sono success. L'API pubblica Pi/Cloudflare rimane bloccata separatamente dalla #147.

**EN:** Character Studio loads the authenticated owner's persisted character through `GET /api/characters/me/appearance`, locks existing name/stats and allows edits only to catalog-sanctioned cosmetics. `PATCH /api/characters/me/appearance` accepts `catalogVersion`, `expectedRevision` and appearance, never a player ID; the JWT principal selects the owner's database row. Migration `202611090001_CharacterAppearanceRevision` adds a bigint revision defaulting to zero, and a single owner+revision CAS `UPDATE` modifies **only cosmetic columns**, rejecting conflicting saves. Repeating an already saved look is idempotent. After a successful commit, the server updates `WorldSimulation` only for a matching character and newer version, increments the world revision and publishes `world.playerUpdated`. Offline/reconnecting clients load the persisted appearance on join. A SignalR transport error cannot retroactively roll back committed data. Commits [`d06b44a`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/d06b44a0c2f6c523e266b1f38b6d6e4668397dc6), [`87b830f`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/87b830f8dc76ad8551176a7928e03376eab7dbef), [`9db7ad0`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/9db7ad0ff2cd8ccd8dd8d1644a1853583fe99852). The first backend CI exposed a missing `using Xunit` in the new PostgreSQL test, fixed in the next commit. [CI #42694635157](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42694635157) and [Live E2E #42694635067](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42694635067) remain under verification, while [Visual #42693836368](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42693836368) and [Pages #42694634612](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42694634612) succeeded. The Raspberry/Cloudflare public health blocker #147 is independent.


## Deploy Pi eseguito, verifica API Cloudflare non accettata / Pi deployed, Cloudflare public API not accepted (#59, #147)

**IT:** Il codice world appearance `ce45942` è stato costruito e distribuito su Raspberry Pi ARM64 con [run #42683874139](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683874139), job **build-arm64 e deploy-pi3 success**. Il controllo GitHub-hosted `verify-public-auth` è però **failure** alle 19:33 UTC: Cloudflare HTTP 502 per health/version/providers/preflight e storico privato, mentre il bridge Pages MSAL risponde HTTP 200. Il job Pi di triage, concluso success alle 19:35 UTC, segnala API/connector up, API healthy, stesso network Docker, origine `http://api:8080/health` HTTP 200 e URL pubblico HTTP 200 osservato *dal namespace connector*; flag OOM=false e restart category none. Le due sonde sono da ambienti e tempi diversi, quindi **non** certificano la stabilità pubblica e non individuano una causa unica. #147 rimane **Blocked**, #59 **In progress**. Verificare configurazione Cloudflare della rotta, tunnel e connector prima di dichiarare il backend produttivo; CI/Visual/Pages/Live E2E di sviluppo verdi non sostituiscono il gate pubblico.

**EN:** World appearance code `ce45942` was built and deployed to Raspberry Pi ARM64 by [run #42683874139](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683874139); **build-arm64 and deploy-pi3 passed**. However, the GitHub-hosted `verify-public-auth` **failed** at 19:33 UTC with Cloudflare HTTP 502 on health/version/providers/preflight/private-history endpoints, while the Pages MSAL bridge returned HTTP 200. Pi triage succeeded at 19:35 UTC: API/connector running, API healthy, shared Docker network, origin `http://api:8080/health` HTTP 200 and public endpoint HTTP 200 **from the connector namespace**, no OOM or restarts reported. The probes ran at different times from different environments: these observations do **not** establish public reliability or prove a specific root cause. #147 stays **Blocked** and #59 **In progress**. Confirm Cloudflare hostname/tunnel/connector routing and keep the strict public gate; passing CI/Visual/Pages/local Live E2E does not replace public production verification.


## Avatar canonici verificati / Canonical character avatars verified (#169, #173, #174)

**IT:** Il primo incremento #169 è **verificato tecnicamente** sui commit [`2e40994`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/2e409943f2fd11e0afe4dda71d63fd3ee6aab875) e [`ce45942`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/ce45942693af714e99ee6dbac3ae024fd85bb820): appearance salvata alla creazione → DTO pubblico catalogVersion allowlist → join/reconnect/delta SignalR → avatar originale Phaser con colori/stili coerenti; fallback per client vecchi/cosmetici invalidi e nessun dato sensibile nello snapshot. La prima [Visual #42682379270](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379270) aveva **74 pass/1 fail** per un'asserzione E2E sul JSON diagnostico dopo reconnect, non per fallimento backend: corretta in `ce45942` senza togliere copertura. Quality gates finali: [CI #42683217408](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217408) **280 Playwright**, backend e Docker success; [Visual #42683217386](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217386) **75/75 pass**, [artifact](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217386/artifacts/13703567799) con screenshot mobile e desktop di due outfit server mock; [Pages #42683217406](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217406) e [Live E2E #42683217263](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217263) success. La review visiva comparativa conferma costumi diversi ma identifica nuova #174 per scala ridotta su widescreen. **#169 resta aperta**: dopo la creazione manca l'API edit/look broadcast post-save #173 e la prova end-to-end di quella funzione su PostgreSQL reale.

**EN:** The first #169 increment is **technically verified** on commits [`2e40994`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/2e409943f2fd11e0afe4dda71d63fd3ee6aab875) and [`ce45942`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/ce45942693af714e99ee6dbac3ae024fd85bb820): creation-time persisted appearance → public versioned allowlist → SignalR join/reconnect/movement delta → original Phaser illustration honoring the saved look. Legacy/invalid values fall back safely and private data never enter the appearance snapshot. Initial [Visual #42682379270](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379270) had **74 passed/1 failed**, caused by an E2E assertion that compared raw double-encoded diagnostic JSON after reconnect; fixed in `ce45942`. Final gates: [CI #42683217408](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217408) **280 Playwright passed**, backend/Docker success; [Visual #42683217386](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217386) **75 passed**, [screenshot artifact](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217386/artifacts/13703567799) captures two server-fixture outfits across viewports; [Pages #42683217406](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217406) and [Live E2E #42683217263](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42683217263) passed. Visual comparison confirms distinct outfits but identified #174 as a future adaptive desktop/ultrawide scale improvement. **#169 stays open**: there is no post-creation appearance edit endpoint or after-commit SignalR broadcast yet (#173), nor a genuine PostgreSQL end-to-end proof of that future workflow.


## Appearance canonica nel mondo / Canonical character cosmetics in multiplayer (#169 — in verifica)

**IT:** Lo snapshot SignalR da `WorldSimulation.Join` ora contiene la nuova struttura `WorldCharacterAppearance` versionata con **solo** hairStyle/hairColor/face/bodyType/skinTone/topColor/bottomStyle/bottomColor/shoeStyle/shoeColor, derivata dal `PlayerCharacter` persistito del proprietario. Il DTO non espone email, account, claims, statistiche, inventario o token, rifiuta valori fuori dal catalogo e accompagna join, reconnect e delta movimento. Il renderer Phaser utilizza davvero questi campi per la silhouette illustrata, capelli, pelle, abbigliamento e scarpe; payload vecchi, null o di versioni sconosciute usano la grafica originale senza crash. I dati non possono essere impostati tramite movement intent. Il mock preview Playwright simula due look server-supplied distinti e controlla l'uso del catalogo sul canvas e dopo reconnect. Code commit [`2e40994`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/2e409943f2fd11e0afe4dda71d63fd3ee6aab875). [CI #42682379053](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379053) backend/Docker success, frontend in esecuzione; [Pages #42682379190](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379190) e [Live #42682378530](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682378530) success, [Visual #42682379270](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379270) da verificare al momento di questa nota. **Limite esplicito:** non esiste ancora un salvataggio appearance post-creazione, dunque la modifica live dopo save e il relativo broadcast sono previsti nella #173. #169 rimane aperta.

**EN:** `WorldSimulation.Join` now includes a versioned public `WorldCharacterAppearance` in SignalR snapshots with **only** persisted hairStyle/hairColor/face/bodyType/skinTone/topColor/bottomStyle/bottomColor/shoeStyle/shoeColor. It is derived from the authenticated owner's stored PlayerCharacter, excluding email, account details, claims, stats, inventory and tokens, and rejects out-of-catalog values. The same cosmetics travel with join, reconnect and movement deltas, never accepting cosmetics from movement intent. Phaser now renders server-sourced hair/skin/outfit/shoes/shape in its original illustrated style, with a safe legacy fallback for absent/unknown versions. Playwright seeds two different cosmetics on the test preview server and checks both peers and reconnect. Commit [`2e40994`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/2e409943f2fd11e0afe4dda71d63fd3ee6aab875). [CI #42682379053](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379053) backend/Docker passed while frontend was running; [Pages #42682379190](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379190) and [Live #42682378530](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682378530) passed, [Visual #42682379270](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42682379270) still unverified at the time of writing. **Known limitation:** post-creation appearance editing and authoritative live broadcast do not yet exist and are tracked in #173. #169 remains open.


## QA grafica e isolamento conclusi / Illustrated world QA and isolation verified (#170, #171, #172)

**IT:** Sul commit grafico `259ba11` i nomi Phaser ora vengono disposti su righe differenti quando i label si sovrappongono; screenshot mobile/desktop verificati mostrano entrambe le etichette. Dopo il fallimento CI precedente [#42675788118](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42675788118) (**278 pass / 1 fail**, probabile interferenza di reset E2E concorrenti), il commit `a7f9026` ha isolato le stanze mock per scenario e aggiunto una prova di indipendenza di join, movimento e reset. Verifica finale: [CI #42677012610](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012610) **success, 280 test Playwright passati**, backend e Docker success; [Visual #42677012318](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012318) **success, 75 test**, [artifact screenshot](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012318/artifacts/13701081421); [Pages #42677012314](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012314) e [Live E2E #42677012724](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012724) **success**. **#170, #171 e #172 chiuse**. Rimangono aperte #154/#155/#158/#165/#167 per QA completa/manuale; #168 richiede RCA del precedente stop scheduling GitHub Actions e #169 appearance server-backed resta pianificata.

**EN:** On graphics commit `259ba11`, Phaser nameplates now occupy separate lanes when their bounds overlap; mobile/desktop screenshots show both labels clearly. Earlier [CI #42675788118](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42675788118) had **278 passed / 1 failed**, consistent with parallel E2E test resets colliding. Commit `a7f9026` isolated test-only mock rooms per scenario and added a regression proving join, movement and reset isolation. Final verification: [CI #42677012610](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012610) **success, 280 Playwright passed**, backend/Docker successful; [Visual #42677012318](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012318) **75 passed**, [screenshot artifact](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012318/artifacts/13701081421); [Pages #42677012314](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012314) and [Live E2E #42677012724](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012724) **success**. **#170, #171 and #172 closed**. #154/#155/#158/#165/#167 remain open for broader human QA; #168 still needs a root-cause analysis of the earlier GitHub Actions scheduling outage, while #169 server-authoritative appearance is planned.


## Mondo mock isolato per QA concorrente / Scoped world mock for parallel QA (#172)

**IT:** La suite CI [#42675788118](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42675788118) del fix nameplate #171 ha superato **278 test** e fallito **1 world-art** (prima tentata verifica di due label, poi dopo retry movimento non confermato). La visual seriale [#42675787653](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42675787653) aveva **74 pass** e screenshot verificati, confermando la separazione visiva dei nomi. Il mock Vite precedente usava un'unica mappa giocatori globale: reset concorrenti di altre suite possono alterare lo scenario. Su main [`a7f9026`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/a7f902677d33738053f0ac3b2d35553d76785046) aggiunto scope facoltativo per ogni scenario **solo** al preview Vite e al client E2E; le suite world-art/visual usano stanze proprie, con test che verifica isolamento di join, movimento e reset. Lo scope di default mantiene i test precedenti invariati. [CI #42677012610](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012610), [Visual #42677012318](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012318) e live #42677012724 ancora in corso; [Pages #42677012314](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012314) già success. Non dichiarare la #172 chiusa prima della suite verde, né #165/#167 con la sola QA automatica.

**EN:** [CI #42675788118](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42675788118) for nameplate #171 passed **278 tests** but failed **one world-art test** (label geometry before retry; world movement on retry). The serial [Visual #42675787653](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42675787653) passed **74** and its screenshots showed separate labels. The previous Vite mock used a single global player map, so other parallel suites' resets could interfere. Commit [`a7f9026`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/a7f902677d33738053f0ac3b2d35553d76785046) introduces optional scenario-scoped rooms **only** in the Vite preview server/E2E client; world-art and visual suites use isolated scopes, with an isolation test for join, movement and reset. The default scope preserves legacy tests. [CI #42677012610](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012610), [Visual #42677012318](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012318) and live #42677012724 remain pending, while [Pages #42677012314](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42677012314) succeeded. #172 must not close without verified green CI, and #165/#167 require independent visual QA.

## Bosco del mondo e viandanti illustrati / Woodland world and illustrated wanderers (#167 — in verifica)

**IT:** Phaser sostituisce i rettangoli giocatore con viandanti 2D procedurali originali: mantello, tunica, occhi, capelli, ombra e dettagli. Palette locali deterministiche e protagonista dorato, etichette più distanti dal personaggio e orientamento orizzontale. Il terreno ora ha regioni boschive, felci, rocce e obelischi ornamentali; i portali conservano le hit area e chiamate server, aggiungendo archi di pietra illustrati. Reduced-motion disattiva il pulsare degli anelli. Il contratto multiplayer non contiene ancora l'appearance canonica: queste forme e i colori **non** pretendono di rappresentare l'aspetto salvato, sono solo grafica. Nessuna collisione, movimento autorevole, premio, asset esterno o regola game cambiata. Commit [`3a602fa`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/3a602fa797873e0f9e00ee373c6c0168888f4b96), nuovi test con due browser + resize; CI/Pages/live E2E e screenshot #165 in verifica.

**EN:** Phaser replaces rectangular player placeholders with original procedural illustrated wanderers: cape, tunic, eyes, hair, shadow and small accessories. Stable local peer palettes, a gold self avatar, name offsets and horizontal facing accompany a woodland world of coherent color regions, ferns, stones and ornamental rune pillars. Portal arches preserve existing click hit areas and server calls. Reduced-motion disables portal ring pulsing. Since the multiplayer snapshot does not include canonical saved appearance, these colors and silhouettes are **not** authoritative player customization. No new collision, server movement, reward, third-party asset or gameplay rule is introduced. Commit [`3a602fa`](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/commit/3a602fa797873e0f9e00ee373c6c0168888f4b96), new two-browser and resize tests; CI/Pages/live E2E plus #165 screenshot verification pending.


## Prove visive game-first e dock mobile / Game-first visual evidence and compact mobile dock (#165, #166)

**IT:** Aggiunta una suite Playwright **su richiesta** (72 combinazioni: 6 schermate × 6 viewport × IT/chiaro + EN/scuro) con screenshot, metriche overflow, host/canvas Phaser, ispezione oggetti e anteprima portali. Dati owner demo sintetici; tutte le chiamate API sono intercettate e le mutation bloccate. Il workflow GitHub Actions archivia report HTML e PNG per review. Sul commit `d286757`: [Visual #42663145378](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145378) **72/72 success**, [CI #42663145583](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145583) **273 Playwright success** con backend/Docker verdi, [Pages #42663145563](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145563) e [Live E2E #42663145621](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145621) **success**. Le immagini hanno scoperto il dock mobile con destinazioni fuori vista: il fix `807a7d7` usa cinque colonne, etichette complete accessibili, icone sempre visibili e quattro regressioni a 320–599px. Nuova [Visual #42664386030](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386030) **72 success** e [Pages #42664386164](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386164) **success**; [CI #42664386272](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386272) **277 Playwright** con backend/Docker success e [Live #42664386458](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386458) **success**. **EN:** Added an **on-demand** Playwright visual suite (72 combinations: six scenes × six viewports × Italian/light and English/dark) capturing screenshots and overflow/Phaser canvas metrics plus inventory inspection and portal preview. All owner data are synthetic fixtures, every API request is intercepted, and writes are denied. A GitHub Actions workflow publishes PNGs and an HTML report for manual inspection. On `d286757`: [Visual #42663145378](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145378) **72 passed**, [CI #42663145583](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145583) **273 Playwright passed** plus successful backend/Docker, [Pages #42663145563](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145563) and [Live E2E #42663145621](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42663145621) **success**. The screenshots exposed clipped mobile dock destinations: fix `807a7d7` shows all five links in equal columns with complete accessible names, icons and four 320–599px regressions. Updated [Visual #42664386030](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386030) **72 passed**, [Pages #42664386164](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386164) **success**; [CI #42664386272](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386272) **277 Playwright passed**, backend/Docker success, [Live #42664386458](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42664386458) **success**. #166 is closed; #165 remains open for manual visual QA. #155, #158 and #165 remain open for human QA; #167 tracks the still-prototypical Phaser avatar and world visuals.


## Console delle soglie / Personal portal rune console (#164 — verificata)

**IT:** La schermata `/portals` diventa una **console di sfida**: le sole assegnazioni restituite dalle API sono mostrate come gateway cards, con conta server-scoped, chiave canonica e avvio esplicito. L'arena distingue **Memorizza / Ripeti / Confermata dal server**, con segni runici originali, pulsanti da gioco, **quattro slot** di risposta e indicatore di avanzamento. Gli slot mostrano **esclusivamente le rune scelte dall'utente**: terminata l'anteprima, la sequenza segreta non compare nella UI. Reset e ripetizione dell'anteprima restano locali; soltanto Conferma invia il tentativo. Danni, successi, premi e consumo del portale restano autorevoli e verificati dal backend. Rimane compatibile con l'overlay Phaser e le route gestite da GameDeck; UI responsiva da 320 a 3440px, IT/EN, tastiera, temi e riduzione del movimento. Nuovi test Playwright per sequenza non rivelata, nessuna POST prima di Conferma e regressioni server. **Verificato** sul commit `a3cdd9f`: [CI #42658490783](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42658490783) **success (273 Playwright)** + backend/Docker, [Pages #42658490905](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42658490905) success e [Live E2E #42658490782](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42658490782) success. La regressione reduced-motion dei gateway MUI è stata corretta. #164 chiusa, QA art direction #158 ancora aperta.

**EN:** `/portals` is now a **game challenge console**: only API-returned assignments appear as gateway cards with server-scoped count, canonical content key and explicit start. The arena distinguishes **Memorize / Repeat / Verified by server**, with original rune symbols, game-style controls, **four entered-answer slots** and a progress meter. The slots display **only runes the user has entered**: when the preview closes, the secret sequence is absent from the UI. Reset and replaying the preview are local actions; only Submit sends an attempt. Damage, success, awards and portal consumption remain authoritative and backend-verified. The page remains compatible with the Phaser overlay and the GameDeck route; responsive 320–3440px, IT/EN, keyboard, themes and reduced-motion. New Playwright regressions verify non-disclosure and no POST before Submit. **Verified** on `a3cdd9f`: [CI #42658490783](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42658490783) **success (273 Playwright)** plus backend/Docker, [Pages #42658490905](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42658490905) success and [Live E2E #42658490782](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42658490782) success. The MUI gateway reduced-motion regression is fixed. #164 closed; manual art-direction QA #158 remains open.


## Studio personaggio / Character creator studio (#163 — verificata)

**IT:** L'editor `/character` diventa una scheda di creazione da gioco con portrait SVG originale, statistiche derivate, **indicatore dei 10 punti disponibili** e due comandi rapidi per raggiungere **Identità e attributi** e **Aspetto e colori**, che spostano scroll e focus nella GameDeck, senza scroll del documento. Su mobile il portrait è compatto e affiancato al riepilogo, e il comando di creazione rimane accessibile. **Prova un altro look** cambia localmente capelli, colori della maglia e pantaloni usando soltanto opzioni del **catalogo API** già validato: non modifica punti, non crea personaggi o ricompense, e il draft continua a persistere localmente. Il server resta responsabile della validazione finale. Nuovi test Playwright 320×568…3440×1440, focus/tastiera, IT/EN, dark/reduced-motion, prova look non-mutante e rispetto delle regole punti. **Verificato** sul commit `61941cd`: [CI #42656434730](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42656434730) success (**263 Playwright**, backend/Docker), [Pages #42656439194](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42656439194) success e [Live E2E #42656434682](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42656434682) success. #163 chiusa, #164 resta in sviluppo.

**EN:** The `/character` editor becomes a game-like creator sheet with the original SVG portrait, derived stats, a **10-point allocation meter**, and quick section commands for **Identity and attributes** and **Appearance and colors**, moving scroll and keyboard focus inside GameDeck rather than scrolling the document. The mobile portrait is compact beside the stat summary and the create action remains available. **Try another look** changes hair and outfit colors only within the local draft, exclusively using the **validated server catalog**: it never modifies points, submits character creation, or grants rewards. The draft remains locally persisted; the server remains authoritative for final validation. New Playwright coverage spans 320×568…3440×1440, keyboard focus, IT/EN, dark/reduced-motion, non-mutating cosmetic trials and point validation. **Verified** on `61941cd`: [CI #42656434730](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42656434730) success (**263 Playwright**, backend/Docker), [Pages #42656439194](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42656439194) success, and [Live E2E #42656434682](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42656434682) success. #163 closed; #164 still under development.


## Rifugio come plancia di gioco / Refuge command dashboard (#162 — verificata)

**IT:** La dashboard Rifugio diventa una plancia con **stato Vuxian immediato**, pulsanti di ingresso a mondo/allenamento e navigazione interna ad attività, salute/riposo, preparazione e cure future. Il riquadro di stato mostra HP e barra **solo quando letti e confermati dalle API della sessione corrente**; ospiti, caricamento, personaggio inesistente (404) e server non disponibile (503) hanno stati distinti e nessun HP fittizio. Per giocatori autenticati salute e preparazione vengono prima dell'elenco delle attività. Le scelte locali continuano a non dare premi; riposo e cooldown restano validati dal server con chiave di idempotenza. Le sezioni scorrono dentro il GameDeck, la barra rimane contestuale su mobile/desktop e la navigazione sposta anche il focus per tastiera. Nuovi controlli Playwright 320×568–3440×1440, API, logout, IT/EN, tema e reduced-motion. **Verificato** su `a00aa08`: [CI #42652724744](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42652724744) success (**253 Playwright**, backend/Docker), [Live E2E #42652724763](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42652724763) success; [Pages #42652724758](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42652724758) **success**, deploy confermato il 9 novembre 2026. #162 chiusa dopo CI, Pages e live E2E verdi; la revisione grafica completa resta in #158/#154.

**EN:** The Refuge becomes a game command dashboard with **immediate Vuxian status**, world/training actions and contextual navigation to activities, health/rest, preparation and future care. Its health summary displays HP and a meter **only when confirmed by the current user's server API**; guests, pending loads, missing characters (404) and service errors (503) have separate states without invented HP. Authenticated players see health and preparation before the activity catalogue. Local practice still grants no rewards; resting/cooldowns remain server-authoritative and idempotent. Long panels scroll inside GameDeck, with a sticky accessible section switcher on mobile/desktop that moves keyboard focus. New Playwright coverage targets 320×568–3440×1440, auth/API errors, logout, IT/EN, themes and reduced motion. **Verified** on `a00aa08`: [CI #42652724744](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42652724744) success (**253 Playwright**, backend/Docker), [Live E2E #42652724763](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42652724763) success; [Pages #42652724758](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42652724758) **success**, deployment confirmed 9 November 2026. #162 closed after successful CI, Pages and live E2E and the full visual review continues in #158/#154.


## Inventario visuale e ispezione / Visual inventory and item inspection (#161 — verificata)

**IT:** Le pile di oggetti diventano una **griglia responsive** di schede con emblemi MUI, quantità posseduta/equipaggiata e azioni di equipaggiamento/cura già esistenti. Il nuovo pulsante **Esamina** apre un dialog accessibile con nome localizzato, codice canonico, quantità e slot derivati esclusivamente dalle letture autorizzate dell'inventario/equipaggiamento. Nessuna rarità, statistica o ricompensa inventata; il dialog si chiude al cambio account e quando viene invalidata la snapshot. Tutti i filtri, ricerca e ordinamento restano invariati. **Verificato**: `027762fa` in [CI #42647968959](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42647968959) (**242 Playwright**, backend e Docker), [E2E reale #42647969288](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42647969288) success. [Pages #42647068144](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42647068144) success sul codice funzionale `47e3e016`; confronto GitHub conferma che `027762fa` modifica **solo il test di logout**. Il [deploy Pages successivo #42647968865](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42647968865), relativo al commit test-only `027762fa`, è poi terminato **success**. #161 chiusa, #158 resta aperta.

**EN:** Inventory stacks now render as a responsive **visual item grid** with MUI emblems, owned/equipped counts and existing equipment/healing actions. A new **Inspect** action opens an accessible dialog with localized name, canonical item key, quantities and slot derived solely from authorized inventory/equipment reads. ## Rune Path: compatibilità mappe pubbliche e fallback offline / Public practice rules compatibility and offline fallback (#153, completata / completed)

**IT:** l'allenamento libero Rune Path confronta la geometria di ciascun labirinto con `GET /api/activities/rune-path/puzzles/{index}`, una sola richiesta pubblica per cambio percorso, senza cookie, JWT, POST o salvataggio di risultati. Controlla versione regole, indice, dimensioni, partenza, arrivo e muri esatti (inclusi doppioni); una risposta fuori contratto o un 502 mostra un messaggio trasparente e lascia il minigioco **completamente giocabile offline**. Le richieste vecchie vengono annullate al cambio percorso e scadono dopo 4 secondi. Indicatore accessibile IT/EN, test Playwright per risposta valida, 502, geometria alterata e race condition. **La compatibilità non è una sessione verificata dal server**, né concede XP, oggetti o ricompense; #73/#100 restano aperte. CI, Playwright, Pages ed E2E verificati con successo.

**EN:** local Rune Path practice compares each board with the public `GET /api/activities/rune-path/puzzles/{index}` endpoint, one public read-only request per board change, without cookies, JWT, POST or saved progress. It strictly checks rules version, puzzle index, size, start, goal and exact wall coordinates (including duplicates). A contract mismatch or HTTP 502 displays a clear warning while leaving practice **fully playable offline**. Previous requests are aborted on board changes and time out after 4 seconds. Added accessible IT/EN status and Playwright coverage for valid response, 502, altered geometry and stale responses. **Geometry compatibility is not a server-verified session** and grants no XP, items or rewards; #73/#100 remain open. CI, Playwright, Pages and live E2E verified successfully.

## Verifica reale budget sonde / Verified real Pi probe budget (#152 closed)

**IT:** su `main` `9d23ae0` la [CI #42623203297](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623203297) è **SUCCESS** (30 test diagnostici Python, backend .NET/PostgreSQL, Docker e Playwright), così come [Pages #42623203660](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623203660) ed [E2E #42623203289](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623203289). Nel [deploy Pi3 #42623826353](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623826353) la build ARM64, il backup PostgreSQL con archive/SHA-256 e l'aggiornamento API sono riusciti; la sonda della rete cloudflared ha confermato **HTTP 200 dall'origine interna** senza il precedente timeout Docker a 15 secondi. Il gate hosted `verify-public-auth` resta **FAILURE**: tutte le rotte API hanno dato Cloudflare HTTP 502, mentre il bridge Microsoft su Pages è 200. Il job Pi di triage, successivo, ha confermato origine interna HTTP 200 e URL pubblico HTTP 200 dalla rete cloudflared circa un minuto dopo, senza OOM o riavvii connector. Questa differenza temporale **non prova la causa** del 502. La #152 è chiusa per la correzione della sonda, ma la #147 e la #149 rimangono aperte; OAuth browser e restore off-device non sono stati verificati.

**EN:** on `main` `9d23ae0`, [CI #42623203297](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623203297) **PASSED** (30 Python diagnostic tests, .NET/PostgreSQL backend, Docker and Playwright); [Pages #42623203660](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623203660) and [E2E #42623203289](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623203289) also passed. [Pi3 deploy #42623826353](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42623826353) successfully built ARM64, validated the PostgreSQL archive/SHA-256 backup and updated the API; the cloudflared namespace probe confirmed **internal origin HTTP 200** without the previous 15-second Docker CLI timeout. GitHub-hosted `verify-public-auth` remained **FAILED**: all API routes returned Cloudflare HTTP 502, while the Microsoft Pages bridge returned 200. The subsequent Pi triage confirmed internal HTTP 200 and public HTTP 200 from the connector namespace about a minute later, with no OOM or connector restarts. Different timing **does not prove the root cause** of the 502. #152 is closed for the diagnostic fix, but #147 and #149 remain open; real browser OAuth and off-device restores have not been verified.

## Timeout Docker delle sonde su Raspberry / Bounded Raspberry Docker probe deadlines (#152, completata / completed)

**IT:** la diagnostica Pi separa il limite per controlli Docker di metadati (15 secondi su `ps` e `inspect`) dal limite complessivo per l'avvio e la rimozione del container di verifica (45 secondi soltanto per `docker run`). I limiti della richiesta HTTP restano brevi: `curl --max-time 6` per l'origine interna, 8 per la route pubblica, `--connect-timeout 3`. Questo evita di classificare immediatamente come timeout una sonda corretta quando il demone Docker parte lentamente sul Raspberry da 1 GiB, senza togliere un limite massimo alla diagnostica. Si mantengono immutati image `--pull=never`, rootfs read-only, capability drop, assenza di volumi, segreti e porte esposte; nessun cambiamento al gate HTTP pubblico. Aggiunti test per timeout separati, scadenza e redazione di output sensibili, oltre al contratto CI. Budget verificato con successo sul Pi reale; **HTTP 502 pubblico #147 non risolto**.

**EN:** Pi diagnostics now separate the Docker metadata command deadline (15 seconds for `ps`/`inspect`) from the end-to-end ephemeral probe container startup and cleanup deadline (45 seconds for `docker run` only). HTTP request bounds stay short: `curl --max-time 6` for the internal origin and 8 for public URL, with `--connect-timeout 3`. This avoids prematurely classifying a healthy probe as a timeout when the Docker daemon starts slowly on a 1 GiB Raspberry, while retaining a hard overall bound. The existing `--pull=never`, read-only root filesystem, dropped capabilities, no mounts, no secrets, no exposed ports and strict public HTTP gate remain unchanged. Tests cover distinct deadlines, expiration and secret-safe output; CI contract updated. Bound verified on the real Pi; **public HTTP 502 #147 remains unresolved**.

## Classificazione 502 verificata sul Pi / Real Pi 502 probe classification (#151 completed, #152 open)

**IT:** il commit `8ea13a8` ha superato [CI #42620764087](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42620764087) (14 test della sonda origine, 14 test di triage, backend .NET/PostgreSQL, Docker e Playwright), [Pages #42620764055](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42620764055) ed [E2E #42620764054](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42620764054). Nel [deploy Pi #42621192685](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42621192685), build/deploy ARM64 e verifica del backup PostgreSQL custom/SHA-256 **riusciti**. La prima sonda da network namespace cloudflared ha indicato **`Docker subprocess timeout`** (15 secondi, nessun HTTP interno misurato): non equivale a un HTTP 502 dell'API. Circa due minuti dopo, il job di triage sul Pi ha verificato API/connector healthy e rete condivisa, **origine `api:8080/health` HTTP 200**, ma **URL pubblico `api.vuxoapi.xyz/health` HTTP 502 dal medesimo namespace**; nessun flag OOM o riavvio connector. La verifica esterna GitHub ha restituito HTTP 502 Cloudflare su tutte le rotte API, mentre il bridge Microsoft Pages rispondeva 200: il gate pubblico resta **fallito**. La #151 è stata chiusa per la diagnostica comprovata; la nuova #152 segue il timeout di startup della sonda temporanea; #147 resta aperta per la connettività pubblica. Nessuna causa Cloudflare esatta è ancora dimostrata.

**EN:** commit `8ea13a8` passed [CI #42620764087](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42620764087) (14 origin and 14 triage Python tests, .NET/PostgreSQL backend, Docker and Playwright), [Pages #42620764055](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42620764055) and [E2E #42620764054](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42620764054). [Pi deploy #42621192685](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42621192685) successfully built/deployed ARM64 and verified the custom PostgreSQL backup and SHA-256. The initial cloudflared-network-namespace probe returned **`Docker subprocess timeout`** (15-second limit, no internal HTTP status measured)—not evidence of an internal API HTTP 502. Approximately two minutes later, the Pi triage observed a healthy API/connector and shared network, **internal `api:8080/health` HTTP 200**, but **public `api.vuxoapi.xyz/health` HTTP 502 from the same namespace**; no Docker OOM or connector restart. Independent GitHub-hosted probes returned Cloudflare HTTP 502 for all API routes while the Microsoft Pages redirect bridge returned HTTP 200, so the **public release gate remains failed**. #151 is closed for proven diagnostic functionality; new #152 tracks the transient Docker probe startup timeout; #147 remains open for public availability. The exact Cloudflare root cause is still unproven.

## Sonde Cloudflare più precise / More precise Cloudflare origin probes (#151, completata / completed)

**IT:** le sonde del Raspberry ora distinguono codici HTTP validi (incluso HTTP 502) da **risoluzione DNS, connessione TCP, timeout curl o Docker, errori TLS, connessione chiusa e impossibilità di avviare il container di prova**. L'output resta un insieme chiuso di categorie e codici HTTP numerici: mai stdout/stderr grezzi, credenziali, indirizzi, header, corpi delle risposte o dati di gioco. La stessa funzione di probe con URL allowlist è condivisa dalla verifica dell'origine `api:8080` e dal confronto con `api.vuxoapi.xyz`. Se la prima sonda interna non conferma HTTP 200, il triage esegue al massimo **una nuova misura dopo la richiesta pubblica**, per evidenziare possibili cambiamenti senza ricorsione né modifiche al runtime. Test offline Python e controllo di contratto CI estesi. Nessuna modifica al gate pubblico, alle route Cloudflare o al database. Il 502 (#147) resta **aperto**; classificazione verificata su CI e Raspberry, con la #147 ancora aperta.

**EN:** the Raspberry probes now distinguish valid HTTP responses (including HTTP 502) from **DNS resolution, TCP connection, curl/Docker timeouts, TLS errors, closed connections and probe-container launch failures**. Output remains a fixed set of categories and numeric HTTP codes—never raw stdout/stderr, credentials, addresses, headers, bodies or gameplay data. Both the internal `api:8080` probe and public `api.vuxoapi.xyz` comparison use the same allowlisted safe helper. If the initial internal probe cannot confirm HTTP 200, triage performs **at most one more sample after the public request** to capture possible changes without recursion or runtime changes. Extended offline Python tests and CI contract assertions. No changes to the strict public gate, Cloudflare routing or database. Public 502 (#147) remains **open**; new classifications verified in CI and on the Raspberry, while #147 remains open.

## Diagnostica automatica 502 verificata / Verified automatic 502 triage (#150 completata / completed)

**IT:** la nuova main `e505beb` ha superato [CI completa #42614156159](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614156159), [GitHub Pages #42614155998](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614155998) ed [E2E con API/PostgreSQL reali #42614156008](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614156008). Nel [deploy Raspberry #42614564669](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614564669), la build ARM64 e il deploy sono passati ma il **gate pubblico è rimasto rosso**: il runner GitHub ha rilevato tutte le rotte API HTTP 502 Cloudflare (health/version/provider/preflight/storici), mentre il bridge Microsoft su Pages era HTTP 200. La diagnostica Pi, avviata automaticamente dopo il fallimento, ha confermato API/connector running e API healthy, nessun flag Docker OOM e nessun riavvio registrato; non ha potuto confermare HTTP 200 dal namespace cloudflared all'origine interna, mentre ha ricevuto HTTP 200 dalla route pubblica **circa un minuto dopo** la misura GitHub. La differenza temporale e di percorso **non dimostra la causa**. La #150 è completata per funzionalità diagnostica; la #147 resta aperta per il 502 intermittente e la #149 resta aperta in attesa dell'accettazione pubblica.

**EN:** on main `e505beb`, [full CI #42614156159](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614156159), [GitHub Pages #42614155998](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614155998), and [real browser/API/PostgreSQL E2E #42614156008](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614156008) all passed. In [Pi deploy #42614564669](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42614564669), ARM64 build and deployment passed but the **public release gate stayed red**: GitHub-hosted diagnostics observed HTTP 502 Cloudflare on all API routes, while Microsoft Pages bridge returned HTTP 200. The conditional Pi triage then confirmed running API/connector and healthy API, with no Docker OOM flag or recorded restarts; the connector namespace origin probe did not confirm internal HTTP 200, while the same namespace reached the public API with HTTP 200 **approximately a minute after** the hosted 502 checks. Different times and paths **do not prove a root cause**. #150 is completed for diagnostic behavior, while #147 stays open for recurring 502 and #149 stays open pending public release acceptance.

## Diagnosi automatica di errore Cloudflare / Automatic Cloudflare failure triage (#150, completata / completed)

**IT:** per analizzare la ricorrenza 502, un fallimento del gate `verify-public-auth` ora attiva **senza ignorare il blocco** due controlli read-only: sul runner GitHub, nove richieste HTTP indipendenti e categorie sanificate; sul Raspberry, una controprova successiva dal network namespace di cloudflared verso l'API interna e l'endpoint pubblico, oltre a flag Docker OOM e categoria dei riavvii. Nessuna stampa di token, URL dinamici, provider ID, IP, body HTTP o log grezzi. Il job Pi parte solo dopo un deploy `main` riuscito e un gate esterno fallito; riutilizza una temporanea immagine API già locale in modalità read-only, senza pull, mount, credenziali o port mapping. Nuovi test Python e contratti CI. Non vengono modificati tunnel, Docker runtime, database o dati dei giocatori. **Non rappresenta una risoluzione del 502** (#147 aperta); test e raccolta su deploy reale verificati.

**EN:** to investigate recurring 502 errors, a failed `verify-public-auth` release gate now triggers **without bypassing the failure** two read-only diagnostics: independent sanitized nine-route HTTP statuses from a GitHub-hosted runner, and subsequent Raspberry checks from cloudflared's network namespace of both internal API and public URL, plus Docker OOM/restart categories. No tokens, dynamic URLs, provider IDs, IPs, HTTP bodies or raw logs are printed. The Pi job requires a successful trusted-main deploy and failed public gate; it reuses an already-local API image in an ephemeral read-only-rootfs container with no pulls, mounts, credentials or exposed ports. Added offline Python tests and CI safety contracts. No changes to Cloudflare configuration, Docker services, databases or player data. **This does not resolve the 502** (#147 remains open); CI and on-failure Pi evidence verified.

## Ricorrenza Cloudflare 502 / Cloudflare 502 recurrence (#147 riaperta / reopened)

**IT:** nel rilascio `8a0ea05`, la [CI completa #42610674554](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42610674554), [Pages #42610674599](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42610674599) e [E2E browser/API/PostgreSQL #42610674523](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42610674523) sono **verdi**. Il [deploy Pi3 #42611174361](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42611174361) ha verificato **prima delle migration** archivio PostgreSQL e checksum SHA-256, API locale healthy e `api:8080/health` HTTP 200 dal network namespace cloudflared; build e deploy verdi. Tuttavia il controllo esterno `verify-public-auth` riceve **HTTP 502 su /health** sia al primo tentativo sia nel rerun del solo job fallito. La #147 è stata riaperta e la #149 resta aperta per l'accettazione pubblica. Il precedente successo pubblico era una misura puntuale, non una prova di stabilità; causa Cloudflare non identificata. Necessaria la verifica del published hostname e del tunnel in dashboard, senza indebolire i controlli o dichiarare OAuth reale funzionante.

**EN:** on `8a0ea05`, full [CI #42610674554](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42610674554), [Pages #42610674599](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42610674599) and [browser/API/PostgreSQL E2E #42610674523](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42610674523) are **green**. [Pi3 deploy #42611174361](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42611174361) verified the PostgreSQL archive and SHA-256 **before migrations**, healthy local API and connector-network-namespace `api:8080/health` HTTP 200; ARM64 build and deployment succeeded. However external `verify-public-auth` returned **HTTP 502 for /health** on its original job and failed-only rerun. #147 was reopened and #149 remains open for public acceptance. An earlier passing public check was a point-in-time observation, not proof of stability; Cloudflare root cause is unconfirmed. Inspect the published hostname/tunnel association in Cloudflare without weakening checks or claiming live OAuth works.

## Backup PostgreSQL verificato prima del deploy / Pre-deploy PostgreSQL backup verification (#149, in verifica / under review)

**IT:** prima della sostituzione del container API e delle eventuali migrazioni EF, il deploy Pi ora verifica il dump PostgreSQL custom **dal file sul disco host** tramite `pg_restore --list` in sola lettura dentro il container PostgreSQL, poi produce e ricontrolla un checksum SHA-256 con permessi privati 0600. Un archivio non leggibile o checksum non verificabile blocca il deploy senza toccare l'API; output di errore senza dati del database. La CI aggiunge casi reali con dump integro, archivio corrotto e manomissione del checksum. **Non sostituisce** restore drill periodici, copia off-device e rollback delle migrazioni: restano nella #59. CI e prova Pi della modifica in verifica.

**EN:** before replacing the API container and applying any EF migrations, the Pi deployment validates the custom PostgreSQL archive **from the host's backup file**, using read-only `pg_restore --list` in the running PostgreSQL container, then generates and verifies a private mode-0600 SHA-256 sidecar. Invalid archives or checksum errors stop deployment before touching the API, with no database content logged. CI adds real intact/corrupt dump and checksum-tampering scenarios. This does **not replace** restore drills, off-device copies or migration rollback testing (#59). New CI and Raspberry acceptance pending.

Issue #142 chiusa con questa evidenza senza unire automaticamente PR divergenti.

There are 35 total branches including `main`; the 34 refs outside the audited manifest, including all five open PR branches, were **preserved**. Issue #142 closed on observed state; no divergent PRs were merged automatically.

Raspberry [ARM64 deploy #42608227225](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42608227225) verified running API/connector, shared Docker network, **HTTP 200** from cloudflared namespace to the API, pre-deploy backup and a **SUCCESS** external `verify-public-auth` gate including health, exact commit, providers/CORS, private 401/preflight and MSAL bridge. Public HTTP 502 did not recur. **Historical root cause is not proven**; #147/#148 closed on demonstrated acceptance, while #59 and #121 remain open for host recovery and real interactive OAuth.

## Diagnostica origine API dal Pi / Pi API origin connectivity (#148, completata / completed)

**IT:** una nuova sonda non distruttiva controlla `http://api:8080/health` **dal network namespace del connector cloudflared**, oltre all'health già locale. Riusa per pochi secondi l'immagine API ARM64 presente sul Pi con root filesystem di sola lettura, nessun download, mount, token, segreto o porta esposta e output fisso PASS/FAIL. Nuovo workflow manuale limitato a `main`, controlli di sicurezza e test Python offline, più diagnostica best-effort dopo il deploy senza disattivare il gate pubblico. Il deploy `6b8bcb6` ha ancora **HTTP 502 pubblico** ([run #42604908677](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42604908677)); la nuova sonda deve ancora essere verificata sul Pi.

**EN:** a non-destructive probe checks `http://api:8080/health` **from the cloudflared connector network namespace**, beyond existing local health. It briefly reuses the locally available ARM64 API image with a read-only root filesystem, no download, mounts, tokens, secrets or exposed ports, and fixed PASS/FAIL messages. Added a trusted-main-only manual workflow, offline Python tests, safety contracts and a best-effort post-deploy check that does not weaken the strict public gate. The `6b8bcb6` deploy still has **public HTTP 502** ([run #42604908677](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42604908677)); the new Pi probe requires actual validation.

## Diagnostica HTTP pubblica indipendente / Independent public HTTP diagnostics (#147, #121 — in verifica / under review)

**IT:** il deploy ARM64 su `1702629` ha confermato API, provider, PostgreSQL e Redis sani sul Raspberry ma il controllo esterno di `/health` è ancora **HTTP 502** anche dopo il riavvio del connector Cloudflare ([run #42602174334](https://github.com/KeyserDSoZe/Vuxo/actions/runs/42602174334)). Portata sulla `main` attuale una diagnostica GitHub Actions **manuale, senza credenziali, read-only**, che consulta indipendentemente health, versione, provider, preflight social, due endpoint Diario anonimi con preflight e redirect Microsoft. Emessi soltanto codice HTTP e categorie ristrette Cloudflare/DNS/TLS/timeout; nessun body, token, ID provider o redirect. Nuovi test Node di classificazione, isolamento e nessuna fuga di segreti. Il gate pubblico 200/401/CORS rimane rigoroso; la causa Cloudflare è ancora da accertare e la issue resta aperta.

**EN:** ARM64 deployment `1702629` confirmed locally healthy API/providers/PostgreSQL/Redis, but the GitHub-hosted external `/health` probe still returned **HTTP 502** after restarting the Cloudflare connector ([run #42602174334](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42602174334)). Ported a **manual, credential-free, read-only** GitHub Actions diagnostic onto current `main`: independently probes health, version, providers, social preflight, anonymous private Journal GET/OPTIONS, and Microsoft redirect bridge. Logs show only numeric status and constrained Cloudflare/DNS/TLS/timeout categories—not bodies, tokens, provider IDs or redirects. Added Node regression tests for classification, continued probes and secrecy. The strict public 200/401/CORS gate is unchanged; Cloudflare root cause remains unverified and the issue stays open.

## Ripresa del tunnel Cloudflare / Cloudflare origin reconnection (#147, in verifica / under review)

**IT:** dopo il deploy ARM64 di `97f8e2e`, l'API sul Raspberry e PostgreSQL risultavano **healthy** localmente, ma due controlli da runner esterno hanno ricevuto **HTTP 502** su `https://api.vuxoapi.xyz/health`. Il nuovo deploy ora riavvia solo il connector `cloudflared` **dopo** il controllo di salute della nuova API, per aggiornare connessioni e risoluzione dell'upstream dopo la sostituzione del container. È una misura da verificare, non una causa certa accertata: restano obbligatori gli smoke pubblici 200/401, CORS e provider. Non vengono toccati database, volumi o token. Nuovi controlli statici su ordine del deploy; esito Pi da verificare.

**EN:** after ARM64 deployment of `97f8e2e`, the Raspberry API and PostgreSQL were locally **healthy**, but two external GitHub-hosted checks received **HTTP 502** from public `/health`. The deploy now restarts only the stateless `cloudflared` connector **after** API health succeeds to refresh origin connections/DNS following container replacement. This is an unverified mitigation, not a proven root cause: public 200/401, CORS and provider smoke checks remain mandatory. Database, volumes and tokens are untouched. Added deploy-order contract tests; Pi outcome pending.

## Database dei test CI senza collisioni / Collision-free CI test database (#146, completato / completed)

**IT:** corretto il job backend della CI che utilizzava la porta PostgreSQL fissa `127.0.0.1:55432` e poteva fallire prima di compilazione e test con `address already in use`. Docker ora assegna una porta loopback libera, verificata prima di esportare la connection string via `GITHUB_ENV`. Nessuna porta PostgreSQL pubblica, nessun segreto nei log; rimangono test .NET/PostgreSQL e backup/restore esistenti. **Verificato:** CI completa con backend .NET/PostgreSQL, backup/restore e Playwright [#42599922739](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42599922739), GitHub Pages [#42599922490](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42599922490), E2E browser/API/PostgreSQL [#42599922353](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42599922353), tutti superati.

**EN:** fixed a backend CI job that used the fixed PostgreSQL host port `127.0.0.1:55432` and could fail before build/tests with `address already in use`. Docker now allocates a free loopback-only port, validated before exporting the test connection string through `GITHUB_ENV`. No public PostgreSQL binding or logged secrets; existing .NET/PostgreSQL and backup/restore checks remain. **Verified:** full .NET/PostgreSQL, backup/restore and Playwright CI [#42599922739](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42599922739), GitHub Pages [#42599922490](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42599922490), browser/API/PostgreSQL E2E [#42599922353](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42599922353), all successful.

## Catalogo attività off-world / Beyond-world activity catalog (#73, prima tranche verificata / first slice verified)

**IT:** prima tranche del motore di attività: manifest pubblico versionato `GET /api/activities/catalog` con tre esercizi realmente disponibili (Memoria, Riflessi, Percorso delle rune) e crafting soltanto pianificato. Tutte le attività hanno **ricompense e progressione disabilitate**. Nuovo endpoint read-only per le tre mappe canoniche Rune Path 4×4 e validatore di dominio bounded (max 32 mosse), con test unitari/API. Nel Rifugio il nuovo catalogo espandibile legge il manifest **solo su richiesta**, è accessibile anche agli ospiti, supporta IT/EN, 320px, retry offline e link alle pratiche. Risposte sconosciute o che dichiarano premi non vengono accettate. Nessun ticket, XP, reward o mutation inventati: ticket persistenti e anti-abuso restano #100/#73. Playwright guest/mobile/dark/keyboard/no Phaser superati nella CI [#42599922739](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42599922739); E2E e Pages verdi. La issue #73 resta aperta per ticket, anti-abuso e premi atomici.

**EN:** the first activity-engine slice publishes versioned read-only `GET /api/activities/catalog` listing three genuinely playable local practices (Memory, Reflexes and Rune Path) plus planned Crafting. **Rewards and progression stay disabled** for all entries. Canonical deterministic 4×4 Rune Path layouts and a bounded 32-step domain validator have API/unit tests. A guest-friendly expandable Refuge catalog fetches only when opened; IT/EN, 320px, offline retry and practice navigation are covered by Playwright. Unknown versions and client-reward claims fail closed. No server run ticket, XP, inventory item or mutation is introduced: persistent authoritative runs and anti-abuse are still tracked by #100/#73. The catalog slice passed CI/Playwright, Pages and live E2E. Issue #73 stays open for server-owned tickets, anti-abuse and atomic rewards.

## Preparazione avventura del Rifugio / Refuge adventure preparation (#114, #117, completata / completed)

**IT:** il Rifugio mostra un pannello **Preparazione all’avventura** con HP confermati, slot Reliquia/Amuleto realmente equipaggiati e stato dell'incontro del portale, letti da API owner-only. Partial fetch failures remain unknown, never "empty" or "clear"; refresh invalidates and reloads all three owner-scoped snapshots, including account switches. Rest uses only the server-confirmed response. Three **Your next step** suggestions offer links to healing review, Inventory, Portals and rune training without client-owned readiness scores or rewards; zero HP never suggests forbidden healing. New Playwright coverage includes guest/404, partial 503/retry, account changes, rest, IT/EN, keyboard/dark/320px. No new server API or migration. **Verified:** CI/Playwright (14 new scenarios) [#42597164221](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42597164221), Pages [#42597164264](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42597164264), API/PostgreSQL E2E [#42597164268](https://github.com/KeyserDSoze/Vuxo/actions/runs/42597164268), all successful. ## Smoke pubblico dello storico personale / Public private-history smoke (#145, in verifica / under review)

**IT:** dopo un deploy ARM64 riuscito, il controllo pubblico da runner GitHub esterno verifica che gli endpoint privati `history/completed` e `history/outcomes` **rifiutino gli ospiti con HTTP 401**, mantengano il CORS canonico di `https://vuxo.quest` e accettino il preflight GET con header Authorization. I controlli ora riportano soltanto il codice HTTP effettivo ricevuto (mai body o dati personali), per distinguere proxy, Cloudflare o route non raggiungibile. ## Portali completati verificati / Verified completed portals (#119, in verifica / under review)

**IT – paginazione stabile:** il Diario carica le pagine successive usando un cursore keyset `nextCursor` restituito dal backend e il parametro opzionale `after` per gli endpoint owner-only dei completamenti e degli esiti. La posizione usa timestamp e ID dell'evento: nuove vittorie più recenti non duplicano o saltano le registrazioni durante lo scorrimento, anche in presenza di timestamp uguali. Le vecchie richieste `page/pageSize` restano valide e il frontend mantiene il fallback per API non ancora aggiornate. Input cursore non valido rifiutato prima del DB. Test API/xUnit, PostgreSQL con inserzioni concorrenti e Playwright IT/EN/320px/tastiera/legacy; nuova CI in verifica. Non è uno snapshot dei backfill storici.

**EN – stable pagination:** the Journal now loads older entries using a versioned `nextCursor` and optional `after` parameter on both authenticated history endpoints. Seek position combines audit timestamp and event ID, keeping page boundaries stable when newer events arrive, including timestamp ties. Legacy `page/pageSize` requests and frontend compatibility fallback remain supported; malformed cursors are rejected before database access. API/xUnit, real PostgreSQL concurrent-insert and IT/EN mobile keyboard/legacy Playwright tests added; fresh CI pending. Historical backfills are not transactionally snapshotted.

**IT – provenienza delle ricompense:** lo storico dei portali ora può mostrare il chip «Ricompensa verificata» solo quando l'assegnazione dell'oggetto è provata da evento causale, grant, proprietario e audit `inventory.item_awarded` coerenti. Niente premi inventati per Guardiani, primi errori, sfide senza conferma o dati legacy incompleti. I read model owner-only aggiungono `rewardItemKey` nullable; indice PostgreSQL additivo e nessuna modifica all'assegnazione degli oggetti. Nuovi test API, integrazione PostgreSQL e Playwright IT/EN/mobile/account-switch; CI della tranche in verifica.

**EN – reward provenance:** portal history now displays a “Verified reward” chip only when a canonical item grant can be traced to the exact completed sequence event through matching owner, grant ID, causation and `inventory.item_awarded` audit. Guardian outcomes, mistakes, missing evidence and incomplete legacy data never receive speculative rewards. Owner-only history DTOs add nullable `rewardItemKey`, backed by an additive PostgreSQL index; item grants remain server-controlled. New API, integration and IT/EN mobile Playwright regressions; fresh CI pending.
